FILE: app/admin-dashboard-secret/login/page.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React, { useState, FormEvent, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const { user, signIn, loading, error } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    // If user is already logged in, redirect to the dashboard
    if (user) {
      router.push('/admin-dashboard-secret');
    }
  }, [user, router]);

  const handleLogin = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    await signIn(email, password);
    // Redirect is handled by the useEffect hook
  };

  // Don't render the form if the user is already logged in and redirecting
  if (user) {
    return <p>Redirecting...</p>; // Or a loading spinner
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md p-8 space-y-6 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow-md">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">Admin Login</h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-6">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Email address</label>
            <input
              id="email"
              name="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 sm:text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Password</label>
            <input
              id="password"
              name="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              required
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 sm:text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              placeholder="Password"
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>

          {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">Error: {error}</p>}

          <div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full flex justify-center py-2 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-md shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'Signing in...' : 'Sign in'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/admin-dashboard-secret/page.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useDashboard } from '@/hooks/useDashboard';
import { getStatusChartData, getScoreDistributionData } from '@/lib/dashboardUtils';
import { createApiHandlers } from '@/lib/apiHandlers';

// Import components
import DashboardHeader from '../components/DashboardHeader';
import StatsCards from '../components/StatsCards';
import DashboardCharts from '../components/DashboardCharts';
import CandidateFilters from '../components/CandidateFilters';
import CandidateManagement from '../components/admin-dashboard/CandidateManagement';
import CalendarSection from '../components/admin-dashboard/CalendarSection';
import DashboardModals from '../components/admin-dashboard/DashboardModals';

function DashboardContent() {
    const {
        user,
        authLoading,
        signOut,
        candidates,
        loadingCandidates,
        activeTab,
        setActiveTab,
        filterStatus,
        setFilterStatus,
        filterName,
        setFilterName,
        filterCity,
        setFilterCity,
        filterCountry,
        setFilterCountry,
        filterNationality,
        setFilterNationality,
        filterJobTitle,
        setFilterJobTitle,
        filterCategory,
        setFilterCategory,
        filterAgeMin,
        setFilterAgeMin,
        filterAgeMax,
        setFilterAgeMax,
        filterSpecialty,
        setFilterSpecialty,
        calendarFilter,
        setCalendarFilter,
        isScoringModalOpen,
        setIsScoringModalOpen,
        isProfileModalOpen,
        setIsProfileModalOpen,
        isJobManagementOpen,
        setIsJobManagementOpen,
        isInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        selectedCandidate,
        setSelectedCandidate,
        statusUpdateLoading,
        setStatusUpdateLoading,
        isScoring,
        setIsScoring,
        isScheduling,
        setIsScheduling,
        scoringError,
        setScoringError,
        toast,
        setToast,
        confirmationModal,
        setConfirmationModal,
        allInterviewEvents,
        filteredInterviewEvents,
        stats,
        filteredCandidates,
        showToast,
        openScoringModal,
        closeScoringModal,
        openProfileModal,
        closeProfileModal,
        openJobManagement,
        closeJobManagement,
        clearAllFilters,
    } = useDashboard();

    const statusChartData = useMemo(() => getStatusChartData(candidates), [candidates]);
    const scoreDistributionData = useMemo(() => getScoreDistributionData(candidates), [candidates]);

    const {
        handleScoreSubmit,
        handleStatusChange,
        handleScheduleInterview,
        handleCancelInterview,
        handleEditCandidate,
    } = createApiHandlers(
        showToast,
        closeScoringModal,
        closeProfileModal,
        setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        setConfirmationModal,
        setStatusUpdateLoading,
        setIsScoring,
        setScoringError,
        setIsScheduling,
        selectedCandidate,
        candidates
    );

    if (authLoading || (user && loadingCandidates)) {
        return (
            <div className="flex justify-center items-center min-h-screen">
                <p>Loading...</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!user) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 p-4 md:p-8">
            <DashboardHeader
                userEmail={user.email || ''}
                onManageJobsClick={openJobManagement}
                onSignOut={signOut}
            />

            <StatsCards stats={stats} candidates={candidates} />

            <DashboardCharts
                statusChartData={statusChartData}
                scoreDistributionData={scoreDistributionData}
            />

            <CandidateFilters
                candidates={candidates}
                filterStatus={filterStatus}
                filterName={filterName}
                filterCity={filterCity}
                filterCountry={filterCountry}
                filterNationality={filterNationality}
                filterJobTitle={filterJobTitle}
                filterCategory={filterCategory}
                filterSpecialty={filterSpecialty}
                filterAgeMin={filterAgeMin}
                filterAgeMax={filterAgeMax}
                onFilterStatusChange={setFilterStatus}
                onFilterNameChange={setFilterName}
                onFilterCityChange={setFilterCity}
                onFilterCountryChange={setFilterCountry}
                onFilterNationalityChange={setFilterNationality}
                onFilterJobTitleChange={setFilterJobTitle}
                onFilterCategoryChange={setFilterCategory}
                onFilterSpecialtyChange={setFilterSpecialty}
                onFilterAgeMinChange={setFilterAgeMin}
                onFilterAgeMaxChange={setFilterAgeMax}
                onClearAllFilters={clearAllFilters}
            />

            <CandidateManagement
                activeTab={activeTab}
                setActiveTab={setActiveTab}
                filteredCandidates={filteredCandidates}
                statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                openProfileModal={openProfileModal}
                openScoringModal={openScoringModal}
                handleStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                filters={{ 
                    status: filterStatus, 
                    name: filterName, 
                    city: filterCity, 
                    country: filterCountry, 
                    nationality: filterNationality,
                    jobTitle: filterJobTitle, 
                    category: filterCategory, 
                    specialty: filterSpecialty,
                    ageMin: filterAgeMin, 
                    ageMax: filterAgeMax 
                }}
            />

            <CalendarSection
                allInterviewEvents={allInterviewEvents}
                filteredInterviewEvents={filteredInterviewEvents}
                calendarFilter={calendarFilter}
                setCalendarFilter={setCalendarFilter}
                openProfileModal={openProfileModal}
            />

            <DashboardModals
                isJobManagementOpen={isJobManagementOpen}
                closeJobManagement={closeJobManagement}
                isScoringModalOpen={isScoringModalOpen}
                closeScoringModal={closeScoringModal}
                selectedCandidate={selectedCandidate}
                handleScoreSubmit={handleScoreSubmit}
                isScoring={isScoring}
                scoringError={scoringError}
                isProfileModalOpen={isProfileModalOpen}
                closeProfileModal={closeProfileModal}
                handleStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                handleCancelInterview={handleCancelInterview}
                statusUpdateLoading={selectedCandidate ? statusUpdateLoading[selectedCandidate.id] || false : false}
                setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen={setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen}
                handleEditCandidate={handleEditCandidate}
                isInterviewSchedulingOpen={isInterviewSchedulingOpen}
                handleScheduleInterview={handleScheduleInterview}
                isScheduling={isScheduling}
                confirmationModal={confirmationModal}
                setConfirmationModal={setConfirmationModal}
                toast={toast}
                setToast={setToast}
            />
        </div>
    );
}

export default function AdminDashboard() {
    return <DashboardContent />;
}

FILE: app/candidate-choices/page.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Header from '../components/candidate-choices/Header';
import Footer from '../components/candidate-choices/Footer';

export default function CandidateChoices() {
  const [candidateEmail, setCandidateEmail] = useState('');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const email = urlParams.get('email');
    if (email) {
      setCandidateEmail(email);
    } else {
      router.push('/');
    }
  }, [router]);

  const handleNewApplication = () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    router.push(`/candidate-form?email=${encodeURIComponent(candidateEmail)}`);
  };

  const handleViewApplications = () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    router.push(`/candidate-dashboard?email=${encodeURIComponent(candidateEmail)}`);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-900 font-sans">
      <Header />
      
      {/* Hero Section */}
      <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="w-full max-w-4xl text-center space-y-8">
          {/* Welcome Content */}
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <h1 className="text-4xl sm:text-6xl font-extrabold text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 leading-tight">
              Welcome Back!
              <span className="block text-2xl sm:text-3xl text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold mt-2">
                What would you like to do?
              </span>
            </h1>
            
            <p className="text-lg sm:text-xl text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 max-w-3xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
              We found existing applications for <strong>{candidateEmail}</strong>. 
              Choose an option below to continue.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Choice Cards */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-8 mt-16">
            {/* New Application Card */}
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none border border-slate-100 dark:border-gray-700 hover:shadow-3xl transition-shadow">
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="text-center space-y-4">
                  <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-[#003A70]/10 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                    <svg className="w-10 h-10 text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                      <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 4v16m8-8H4" />
                    </svg>
                  </div>
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400">New Application</h2>
                  <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Submit a fresh application for a new job opportunity. 
                    Fill out the complete application form with your latest information.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={handleNewApplication}
                  disabled={isLoading}
                  className="w-full px-8 py-4 bg-[#003A70] text-white font-bold text-lg tracking-wide rounded-xl shadow-lg hover:bg-[#002B54] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-[#003A70] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all transform hover:scale-[1.02]"
                >
                  {isLoading ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2">
                      <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                      <span>Loading...</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    'Start New Application'
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* View Applications Card */}
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none border border-slate-100 dark:border-gray-700 hover:shadow-3xl transition-shadow">
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="text-center space-y-4">
                  <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-green-100 dark:bg-green-900/50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                    <svg className="w-10 h-10 text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                      <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" />
                    </svg>
                  </div>
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Review Applications</h2>
                  <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    View and manage your existing applications. 
                    Check application status, interview schedules, and download documents.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={handleViewApplications}
                  disabled={isLoading}
                  className="w-full px-8 py-4 bg-green-600 text-white font-bold text-lg tracking-wide rounded-xl shadow-lg hover:bg-green-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-green-600 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all transform hover:scale-[1.02]"
                >
                  {isLoading ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2">
                      <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                      <span>Loading...</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    'View My Applications'
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Additional Info */}
          <div className="mt-12 p-6 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 rounded-xl border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30">
            <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
              <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />

FILE: app/candidate-dashboard/page.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { useCandidateDashboard } from '@/hooks/useCandidateDashboard';
import ApplicationStatus from '../components/candidate-dashboard/ApplicationStatus';
import InterviewDetails from '../components/candidate-dashboard/InterviewDetails';
import DashboardHeader from '../components/candidate-dashboard/DashboardHeader';
import DashboardFooter from '../components/common/Footer';
import WelcomeSection from '../components/candidate-dashboard/WelcomeSection';
import HelpSection from '../components/candidate-dashboard/HelpSection';

export default function CandidateDashboard() {
  const {
    candidateEmail,
    applications,
    isLoading,
    error,
    handleNewApplication,
    formatDate,
  } = useCandidateDashboard();

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-[#003A70] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-4"></div>
          <p className="text-slate-600">Loading your applications...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-900 font-sans">
      <DashboardHeader />
      
      <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-start py-12 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="w-full max-w-6xl space-y-8">
          <WelcomeSection
            candidateEmail={candidateEmail}
            handleNewApplication={handleNewApplication}
          />
          
          {error && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          <ApplicationStatus applications={applications} formatDate={formatDate} />
          
          <InterviewDetails applications={applications} formatDate={formatDate} />

          <HelpSection />
        </div>
      </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import CandidateFilters from '../CandidateFilters';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

// Mock candidates data for testing
const mockCandidates: Candidate[] = [
  { id: '1', name: 'Alice Smith', city: 'New York', country: 'USA', nationality: 'American', jobTitleText: 'Software Engineer', jobCategory: 'IT', specialty: 'Frontend', age: 28, status: 'submitted' },
  { id: '2', name: 'Bob Johnson', city: 'London', country: 'UK', nationality: 'British', jobTitleText: 'Data Scientist', jobCategory: 'Data', specialty: 'Machine Learning', age: 35, status: 'interview_scheduled' },
];

describe('CandidateFilters', () => {
  // Mock functions for filter change handlers
  const onFilterStatusChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterNameChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterCityChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterCountryChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterNationalityChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterJobTitleChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterCategoryChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterSpecialtyChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterAgeMinChange = jest.fn();
  const onFilterAgeMaxChange = jest.fn();
  const onClearAllFilters = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    candidates: mockCandidates,
    filterStatus: 'all',
    filterName: '',
    filterCity: '',
    filterCountry: '',
    filterNationality: '',
    filterJobTitle: '',
    filterCategory: '',
    filterSpecialty: '',
    filterAgeMin: '',
    filterAgeMax: '',
    onFilterStatusChange,
    onFilterNameChange,
    onFilterCityChange,
    onFilterCountryChange,
    onFilterNationalityChange,
    onFilterJobTitleChange,
    onFilterCategoryChange,
    onFilterSpecialtyChange,
    onFilterAgeMinChange,
    onFilterAgeMaxChange,
    onClearAllFilters,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    // Clear mock function calls before each test
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('renders all filter inputs correctly', () => {
    render(<CandidateFilters {...defaultProps} />);

    // Check for main filter inputs
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Filter by name...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select city...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select country...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select nationality...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select category...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select specialty...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select job title...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Min age...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Max age...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Filter by Status')).toBeInTheDocument();

    // Check for quick filter buttons
    expect(screen.getByText('Status:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Popular Specialties:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Nationalities:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Popular Cities:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Popular Countries:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Job Categories:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Job Positions:')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls onFilterNameChange when typing in the name filter', () => {
    render(<CandidateFilters {...defaultProps} />);
    const nameInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText('Filter by name...');
    fireEvent.change(nameInput, { target: { value: 'Alice' } });
    expect(onFilterNameChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Alice');
  });

  it('calls onClearAllFilters when the "Clear All Filters" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<CandidateFilters {...defaultProps} />);
    const clearButton = screen.getByText('Clear All Filters');
    fireEvent.click(clearButton);
    expect(onClearAllFilters).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('calls onFilterStatusChange when a status quick filter is clicked', () => {
    render(<CandidateFilters {...defaultProps} />);
    const submittedButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Submitted/ });
    fireEvent.click(submittedButton);
    expect(onFilterStatusChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('submitted');
  });
});

FILE: app/components/__tests__/CandidateTable.test.tsx
============================================================
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import CandidateTable from '../CandidateTable';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { useCandidateTable } from '@/hooks/useCandidateTable';

jest.mock('@/hooks/useCandidateTable', () => ({
    useCandidateTable: jest.fn(),
}));

describe('CandidateTable', () => {
  const mockCandidates: Candidate[] = [
    { id: '1', fullName: 'Alice Smith', email: 'alice@example.com', status: 'submitted', submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() } },
    { id: '2', fullName: 'Bob Johnson', email: 'bob@example.com', status: 'interview_scheduled', submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() } },
  ];

  const defaultProps = {
    candidates: mockCandidates,
    statusUpdateLoading: {},
    onViewCandidate: jest.fn(),
    onScoreCandidate: jest.fn(),
    onStatusChange: jest.fn(),
    onEditCandidate: jest.fn(),
    filters: {},
  };

  const mockUseCandidateTable = useCandidateTable as jest.Mock;

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockUseCandidateTable.mockClear();
  });

  it('renders the table with candidate data', () => {
    mockUseCandidateTable.mockReturnValue({
        sortedCandidates: mockCandidates,
        sortField: 'firstName',
        sortDirection: 'asc',
        handleSort: jest.fn(),
        splitName: (name: string) => ({ firstName: name.split(' ')[0], lastName: name.split(' ')[1] }),
        calculateAge: () => 30,
        isExporting: false,
        handleExport: jest.fn(),
    });
    render(<CandidateTable {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Alice Smith')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Bob Johnson')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the "No candidates found" message when there are no candidates', () => {
    mockUseCandidateTable.mockReturnValue({
        sortedCandidates: [],
        sortField: 'firstName',
        sortDirection: 'asc',
        handleSort: jest.fn(),
        splitName: (name: string) => ({ firstName: name.split(' ')[0], lastName: name.split(' ')[1] }),
        calculateAge: () => 30,
        isExporting: false,
        handleExport: jest.fn(),
    });
    render(<CandidateTable {...defaultProps} candidates={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No candidates found')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls handleSort when a table header is clicked', () => {
    const handleSort = jest.fn();
    mockUseCandidateTable.mockReturnValue({
        sortedCandidates: mockCandidates,
        sortField: 'firstName',
        sortDirection: 'asc',
        handleSort,
        splitName: (name: string) => ({ firstName: name.split(' ')[0], lastName: name.split(' ')[1] }),
        calculateAge: () => 30,
        isExporting: false,
        handleExport: jest.fn(),
    });

    render(<CandidateTable {...defaultProps} />);
    const nameHeader = screen.getByText('Name');
    fireEvent.click(nameHeader);
    expect(handleSort).toHaveBeenCalledWith('firstName');
  });
});

FILE: app/components/__tests__/DashboardCharts.test.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import DashboardCharts from '../DashboardCharts';
import { ScoreDistributionData, StatusChartData } from '@/types';

// Mock the recharts library
jest.mock('recharts', () => {
  const OriginalModule = jest.requireActual('recharts');
  return {
    ...OriginalModule,
    ResponsiveContainer: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="responsive-container">{children}</div>,
    PieChart: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="pie-chart">{children}</div>,
    Pie: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="pie">{children}</div>,
    Cell: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="cell">{children}</div>,
    Tooltip: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="tooltip">{children}</div>,
    Legend: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="legend">{children}</div>,
    BarChart: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="bar-chart">{children}</div>,
    Bar: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="bar">{children}</div>,
    XAxis: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="x-axis">{children}</div>,
    YAxis: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="y-axis">{children}</div>,
    CartesianGrid: ({ children }) => <div data-testid="cartesian-grid">{children}</div>,
  };
});

describe('DashboardCharts', () => {
  const mockStatusData: StatusChartData = [
    { name: 'Submitted', value: 10 },
    { name: 'Scheduled', value: 5 },
    { name: 'Rejected', value: 2 },
  ];

  const mockScoreData: ScoreDistributionData = [
    { name: '0-20', count: 1 },
    { name: '21-40', count: 2 },
    { name: '41-60', count: 5 },
    { name: '61-80', count: 8 },
    { name: '81-100', count: 4 },
  ];

  it('renders the chart titles', () => {
    render(<DashboardCharts statusChartData={mockStatusData} scoreDistributionData={mockScoreData} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Candidate Status Distribution')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('AI Score Distribution')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the charts when data is provided', () => {
    render(<DashboardCharts statusChartData={mockStatusData} scoreDistributionData={mockScoreData} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('pie-chart')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('bar-chart')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the "no data" message for the status chart when no data is provided', () => {
    render(<DashboardCharts statusChartData={[]} scoreDistributionData={mockScoreData} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No candidate data available')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the "no data" message for the score chart when no data is provided', () => {
    const emptyScoreData = [
      { name: '0-20', count: 0 },
      { name: '21-40', count: 0 },
      { name: '41-60', count: 0 },
      { name: '61-80', count: 0 },
      { name: '81-100', count: 0 },
    ];
    render(<DashboardCharts statusChartData={mockStatusData} scoreDistributionData={emptyScoreData} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No scoring data available')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/__tests__/JobManagement.test.tsx
============================================================
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import JobManagement from '../JobManagement';
import { Job } from '@/types';

// Mock the fetch API
global.fetch = jest.fn();
// Mock console.error
console.error = jest.fn();

describe('JobManagement', () => {
  const onClose = jest.fn();

  const mockJobs: Job[] = [
    { id: '1', title: 'Software Engineer', description: 'Develop amazing things', category: 'IT', specialties: ['Frontend'], isActive: true, createdAt: { toDate: () => new Date() } },
    { id: '2', title: 'Data Scientist', description: 'Analyze data', category: 'Data', specialties: ['Machine Learning'], isActive: false, createdAt: { toDate: () => new Date() } },
  ];

  beforeEach(() => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockClear();
    (console.error as jest.Mock).mockClear();
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('renders the component when open', async () => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({ jobs: mockJobs }),
    });

    render(<JobManagement isOpen={true} onClose={onClose} />);

    expect(screen.getByText('Job Management')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText('Software Engineer')).toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it('renders the "Add New Job" button', async () => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({ jobs: [] }),
    });

    render(<JobManagement isOpen={true} onClose={onClose} />);

    expect(await screen.findByTestId('add-job-button')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('handles error when fetching jobs', async () => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Failed to fetch jobs'));

    render(<JobManagement isOpen={true} onClose={onClose} />);

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText('Failed to fetch jobs')).toBeInTheDocument());
  });
});

FILE: app/components/__tests__/ScoringModal.test.tsx
============================================================
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import ScoringModal from '../ScoringModal';
import { Candidate, Job } from '@/types';

// Mock the fetch API
global.fetch = jest.fn();

describe('ScoringModal', () => {
  const onClose = jest.fn();
  const onSubmit = jest.fn();

  const mockCandidate: Candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'John Doe',
    email: 'john.doe@example.com',
  };

  const mockJobs: Job[] = [
    { id: '1', title: 'Software Engineer', description: 'Develop amazing things', category: 'IT', specialties: ['Frontend'], isActive: true },
    { id: '2', title: 'Data Scientist', description: 'Analyze data', category: 'Data', specialties: ['Machine Learning'], isActive: true },
  ];

  beforeEach(() => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockClear();
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('renders the modal with the candidate name', async () => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({ jobs: mockJobs }),
    });

    render(<ScoringModal isOpen={true} onClose={onClose} candidate={mockCandidate} onSubmit={onSubmit} isLoading={false} error={null} />);

    expect(screen.getByText('Score Candidate: John Doe')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText('Software Engineer')).toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it('shows the job description when a job is selected', async () => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({ jobs: mockJobs }),
    });

    render(<ScoringModal isOpen={true} onClose={onClose} candidate={mockCandidate} onSubmit={onSubmit} isLoading={false} error={null} />);

    const select = await screen.findByRole('combobox');
    fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: '1' } });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText('Develop amazing things')).toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it('calls onSubmit with the correct job details when the form is submitted', async () => {
    (fetch as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce({
      ok: true,
      json: async () => ({ jobs: mockJobs }),
    });

    render(<ScoringModal isOpen={true} onClose={onClose} candidate={mockCandidate} onSubmit={onSubmit} isLoading={false} error={null} />);

    const select = await screen.findByRole('combobox');
    fireEvent.change(select, { target: { value: '1' } });

    const submitButton = screen.getByText('Score Candidate');
    fireEvent.click(submitButton);

    await waitFor(() => expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 'Software Engineer', 'Develop amazing things'));
  });
});

FILE: app/components/__tests__/StatsCards.test.tsx
============================================================
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import StatsCards from '../StatsCards';
import { Stats, Candidate } from '@/types';

describe('StatsCards', () => {
  const mockStats: Stats = {
    total: 10,
    submitted: 8,
    scheduled: 5,
    approved: 3,
    rejected: 2,
  };

  const mockCandidates: Candidate[] = [
    { id: '1', name: 'Alice', score: 85, status: 'approved' },
    { id: '2', name: 'Bob', score: 75, status: 'scheduled' },
    { id: '3', name: 'Charlie', score: 90, status: 'approved' },
    { id: '4', name: 'David', score: null, status: 'submitted' },
  ];

  it('renders the main stats cards correctly', () => {
    render(<StatsCards stats={mockStats} candidates={mockCandidates} />);

    const totalCandidatesCard = screen.getByText('Total Candidates').closest('div');
    expect(within(totalCandidatesCard).getByText('10')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const submittedCard = screen.getByText('Submitted').closest('div');
    expect(within(submittedCard).getByText('8')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const scheduledCard = screen.getByText('Interview Scheduled').closest('div');
    expect(within(scheduledCard).getByText('5')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const rejectedCard = screen.getByText('Rejected').closest('div');
    expect(within(rejectedCard).getByText('2')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calculates and renders the secondary stats correctly', () => {
    render(<StatsCards stats={mockStats} candidates={mockCandidates} />);

    const averageScoreCard = screen.getByText('Average AI Score').closest('div');
    expect(within(averageScoreCard).getByText('83.3')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const highScorersCard = screen.getByText('High Scorers (80+)').closest('div');
    expect(within(highScorersCard).getByText('2')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const conversionRateCard = screen.getByText('Conversion Rate').closest('div');
    expect(within(conversionRateCard).getByText('62.5%')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const interviewSuccessCard = screen.getByText('Interview Success').closest('div');
    expect(within(interviewSuccessCard).getByText('60.0%')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/admin-dashboard/__tests__/CalendarSection.test.tsx
============================================================

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import CalendarSection from '../CalendarSection';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';
import type { InterviewEvent } from '@/types';

// Mock react-big-calendar
jest.mock('react-big-calendar', () => ({
  Calendar: ({ events, onSelectEvent }: { events: InterviewEvent[], onSelectEvent: (event: InterviewEvent) => void }) => (
    <div data-testid="mock-calendar">
      {events.map(event => (
        <div key={event.title} onClick={() => onSelectEvent(event)}>
          {event.title}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  ),
  dateFnsLocalizer: jest.fn(),
}));

const mockOpenProfileModal = jest.fn();
const mockSetCalendarFilter = jest.fn();

const mockAllInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[] = [
  {
    title: 'Interview with John Doe',
    start: new Date(2024, 6, 20, 10, 0, 0),
    end: new Date(2024, 6, 20, 11, 0, 0),
    resource: { fullName: 'John Doe' },
    isCompleted: false,
  },
  {
    title: 'Interview with Jane Smith',
    start: new Date(2024, 6, 21, 14, 0, 0),
    end: new Date(2024, 6, 21, 15, 0, 0),
    resource: { fullName: 'Jane Smith' },
    isCompleted: true,
  },
];

const defaultProps = {
  allInterviewEvents: mockAllInterviewEvents,
  filteredInterviewEvents: mockAllInterviewEvents,
  calendarFilter: 'all' as const,
  setCalendarFilter: mockSetCalendarFilter,
  openProfileModal: mockOpenProfileModal,
};

describe('CalendarSection', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('should render the calendar with events', () => {
    render(<CalendarSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Interview Schedule')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('mock-calendar')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Interview with John Doe')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Interview with Jane Smith')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call setCalendarFilter when filter buttons are clicked', () => {
    render(<CalendarSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Upcoming/ }));
    expect(mockSetCalendarFilter).toHaveBeenCalledWith('upcoming');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Completed/ }));
    expect(mockSetCalendarFilter).toHaveBeenCalledWith('completed');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /All/ }));
    expect(mockSetCalendarFilter).toHaveBeenCalledWith('all');
  });

  it('should call openProfileModal when an event is clicked', () => {
    render(<CalendarSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Interview with John Doe'));
    expect(mockOpenProfileModal).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ fullName: 'John Doe' });
  });

  it('should display no interviews message when there are no events', () => {
    render(<CalendarSection {...defaultProps} filteredInterviewEvents={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/No interviews/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/admin-dashboard/CalendarSection.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { Calendar, dateFnsLocalizer, Event as CalendarEvent } from 'react-big-calendar';
import { format } from 'date-fns/format';
import { parse } from 'date-fns/parse';
import { startOfWeek } from 'date-fns/startOfWeek';
import { getDay } from 'date-fns/getDay';
import { enUS } from 'date-fns/locale/en-US';
import 'react-big-calendar/lib/css/react-big-calendar.css';
import type { InterviewEvent } from '@/types';

const locales = { 'en-US': enUS };
const localizer = dateFnsLocalizer({ format, parse, startOfWeek, getDay, locales });

interface CalendarSectionProps {
    allInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[];
    filteredInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[];
    calendarFilter: 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed';
    setCalendarFilter: (filter: 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed') => void;
    openProfileModal: (candidate: any) => void;
}

const eventStyleGetter = (event: InterviewEvent) => {
    const style: React.CSSProperties = {
        borderRadius: '4px',
        opacity: 0.8,
        color: 'white',
        border: '0px',
        display: 'block',
        fontSize: '12px',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
    };

    if (event.isCompleted) {
        style.backgroundColor = '#10B981'; // Green
        style.borderLeft = '4px solid #047857'; // Darker green border
        style.opacity = 0.9;
    } else {
        style.backgroundColor = '#3B82F6'; // Blue
        style.borderLeft = '4px solid #1D4ED8'; // Darker blue border
    }

    return { style };
};

const CalendarSection: React.FC<CalendarSectionProps> = ({
    allInterviewEvents,
    filteredInterviewEvents,
    calendarFilter,
    setCalendarFilter,
    openProfileModal,
}) => {
    return (
        <section className="mt-8">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mb-6 gap-4">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Interview Schedule</h3>
                    
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 mr-2">Filter:</span>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCalendarFilter('all')}
                            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                                calendarFilter === 'all' 
                                    ? 'bg-gray-600 text-white border-gray-600' 
                                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-600'
                            }`}
                        >
                            All ({allInterviewEvents.length})
                        </button>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCalendarFilter('upcoming')}
                            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                                calendarFilter === 'upcoming' 
                                    ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' 
                                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-300 border-blue-300 dark:border-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/50'
                            }`}
                        >
                            Upcoming ({allInterviewEvents.filter(e => !e.isCompleted).length})
                        </button>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCalendarFilter('completed')}
                            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                                calendarFilter === 'completed' 
                                    ? 'bg-green-600 text-white border-green-600' 
                                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-green-600 dark:text-green-300 border-green-300 dark:border-green-600 hover:bg-green-50 dark:hover:bg-green-900/50'
                            }`}
                        >
                            Completed ({allInterviewEvents.filter(e => e.isCompleted).length})
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center space-x-6 mb-4 p-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700/50 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-blue-500 rounded border-l-4 border-blue-700"></div>
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Upcoming Interviews</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-green-500 rounded border-l-4 border-green-700"></div>
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Completed Interviews</span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ height: '500px' }} className="dark-calendar-theme">
                    <Calendar
                        localizer={localizer}
                        events={filteredInterviewEvents}
                        startAccessor="start"
                        endAccessor="end"
                        style={{ height: '100%' }}
                        views={['month', 'week', 'day']}
                        defaultView="month"
                        popup
                        eventPropGetter={eventStyleGetter}
                        onSelectEvent={(event) => openProfileModal(event.resource)}
                        tooltipAccessor={(event: CalendarEvent) => {
                            const interviewEvent = event as InterviewEvent;
                            const candidate = interviewEvent.resource;
                            const status = interviewEvent.isCompleted ? 'Completed' : 'Scheduled';
                            const time = format(interviewEvent.start as Date, 'PPpp');
                            return `${status}: ${candidate.fullName} - ${time}`;
                        }}
                    />
                </div>

                {filteredInterviewEvents.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                        <svg className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mb-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 7V3a1 1 0 011-1h6a1 1 0 011 1v4h2a1 1 0 011 1v10a1 1 0 01-1 1H5a1 1 0 01-1-1V8a1 1 0 011-1h3z" />
                        </svg>
                        <p className="text-lg font-medium">No interviews {calendarFilter === 'all' ? '' : calendarFilter}</p>
                        <p className="text-sm">
                            {calendarFilter === 'all' && 'Schedule some interviews to see them here.'}
                            {calendarFilter === 'upcoming' && 'No upcoming interviews scheduled.'}
                            {calendarFilter === 'completed' && 'No completed interviews yet.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </section>
    );
};

export default CalendarSection;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import Tabs from '../ui/Tabs';
import CandidateTable from '../CandidateTable';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import ExportButtons from '../candidate-table/ExportButtons';
import { useCandidateTable } from '@/hooks/useCandidateTable';


interface CandidateManagementProps {
    activeTab: string;
    setActiveTab: (tab: string) => void;
    filteredCandidates: Candidate[];
    statusUpdateLoading: Record<string, boolean>;
    openProfileModal: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    openScoringModal: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    handleStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string) => void;
    filters: {
        status: string;
        name: string;
        city: string;
        country: string;
        nationality: string;
        jobTitle: string;
        category: string;
        specialty: string;
        ageMin: string;
        ageMax: string;
    };
}

const CandidateManagement: React.FC<CandidateManagementProps> = ({
    activeTab,
    setActiveTab,
    filteredCandidates,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    openProfileModal,
    openScoringModal,
    handleStatusChange,
    filters,
}) => {
    const { isExporting, handleExport } = useCandidateTable(filteredCandidates, filters);

    return (
        <div className="mt-8 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Candidate List</h2>
                <ExportButtons isExporting={isExporting} handleExport={handleExport} />
            </div>
            <Tabs
                activeTab={activeTab}
                onTabChange={setActiveTab}
                tabs={[
                    { label: 'Main', value: 'main' },
                    { label: 'Approved', value: 'approved' },
                    { label: 'Rejected', value: 'rejected' },
                ]}
            >
                <CandidateTable
                    candidates={filteredCandidates}
                    statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                    onViewCandidate={openProfileModal}
                    onScoreCandidate={openScoringModal}
                    onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                    filters={filters}
                />
            </Tabs>
        </div>
    );
};

export default CandidateManagement;

FILE: app/components/admin-dashboard/DashboardModals.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import JobManagement from '../JobManagement';
import ScoringModal from '../ScoringModal';
import CandidateProfileModal from '../CandidateProfileModal';
import InterviewSchedulingModal from '../InterviewSchedulingModal';
import ConfirmationModal from '../ui/ConfirmationModal';
import Toast from '../ui/Toast';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface DashboardModalsProps {
    isJobManagementOpen: boolean;
    closeJobManagement: () => void;
    isScoringModalOpen: boolean;
    closeScoringModal: () => void;
    selectedCandidate: Candidate | null;
    handleScoreSubmit: (jobId: string, jobTitle: string, jobDescription: string) => Promise<void>;
    isScoring: boolean;
    scoringError: string | null;
    isProfileModalOpen: boolean;
    closeProfileModal: () => void;
    handleStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string, interviewTimestampISO?: string) => Promise<void>;
    handleCancelInterview: (candidateId: string, reason?: string) => Promise<void>;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
    setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
    handleEditCandidate: (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => Promise<void>;
    isInterviewSchedulingOpen: boolean;
    handleScheduleInterview: (
        candidateId: string, 
        date: string, 
        time: string, 
        duration: number,
        adminEmail: string,
        notes?: string
    ) => Promise<void>;
    isScheduling: boolean;
    confirmationModal: {
        isOpen: boolean;
        title: string;
        message: string;
        onConfirm: () => void;
    };
    setConfirmationModal: (modal: any) => void;
    toast: {
        message: string;
        type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';
        isVisible: boolean;
    };
    setToast: (toast: any) => void;
}

const DashboardModals: React.FC<DashboardModalsProps> = ({
    isJobManagementOpen,
    closeJobManagement,
    isScoringModalOpen,
    closeScoringModal,
    selectedCandidate,
    handleScoreSubmit,
    isScoring,
    scoringError,
    isProfileModalOpen,
    closeProfileModal,
    handleStatusChange,
    handleCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
    handleEditCandidate,
    isInterviewSchedulingOpen,
    handleScheduleInterview,
    isScheduling,
    confirmationModal,
    setConfirmationModal,
    toast,
    setToast,
}) => {
    return (
        <>
            <JobManagement
                isOpen={isJobManagementOpen}
                onClose={closeJobManagement}
            />

            <ScoringModal
                isOpen={isScoringModalOpen}
                onClose={closeScoringModal}
                candidate={selectedCandidate}
                onSubmit={handleScoreSubmit}
                isLoading={isScoring}
                error={scoringError}
            />

            <CandidateProfileModal
                isOpen={isProfileModalOpen}
                onClose={closeProfileModal}
                candidate={selectedCandidate}
                onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                onCancelInterview={handleCancelInterview}
                statusUpdateLoading={selectedCandidate ? statusUpdateLoading : false}
                onScheduleInterview={() => setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen(true)}
                onEditCandidate={handleEditCandidate}
            />

            <InterviewSchedulingModal
                isOpen={isInterviewSchedulingOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen(false)}
                candidate={selectedCandidate}
                onSchedule={handleScheduleInterview}
                isLoading={isScheduling}
            />

            <ConfirmationModal
                isOpen={confirmationModal.isOpen}
                title={confirmationModal.title}
                message={confirmationModal.message}
                onConfirm={confirmationModal.onConfirm}
                onCancel={() => setConfirmationModal((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, isOpen: false }))}
            />

            <Toast
                message={toast.message}
                type={toast.type}
                isVisible={toast.isVisible}
                onClose={() => setToast((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, isVisible: false }))}
            />
        </>
    );
};

export default DashboardModals;

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import ApplicationStatus from '../ApplicationStatus';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ApplicationStatus', () => {
  const formatDateMock = jest.fn((date) => new Date(date.toDate()).toLocaleDateString());

  const applications = [
    {
      id: '1',
      fullName: 'Test User',
      email: 'test@example.com',
      status: 'submitted',
      jobTitleText: 'Software Engineer',
      jobCategory: 'IT',
      specialty: 'Frontend',
      score: 85,
      submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-01-01') },
      cvUrl: 'http://example.com/cv.pdf',
      passportUrl: 'http://example.com/passport.pdf',
      photoUrl: 'http://example.com/photo.jpg',
    },
    {
      id: '2',
      fullName: 'Test User 2',
      email: 'test2@example.com',
      status: 'interview_scheduled',
      jobTitleText: 'Product Manager',
      jobCategory: 'Product',
      specialty: 'Mobile',
      score: 90,
      submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-02-01') },
      interviewDate: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-03-01') },
      meetLink: 'http://meet.google.com',
      cvUrl: 'http://example.com/cv2.pdf',
      passportUrl: 'http://example.com/passport2.pdf',
    },
  ];

  it('should render the "No Applications Yet" message when there are no applications', () => {
    render(<ApplicationStatus applications={[]} formatDate={formatDateMock} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No Applications Yet')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the application details correctly when there are applications', () => {
    render(<ApplicationStatus applications={applications} formatDate={formatDateMock} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Software Engineer')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Product Manager')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('IT')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Frontend')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('85/100')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('90/100')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the correct number of applications', () => {
    render(<ApplicationStatus applications={applications} formatDate={formatDateMock} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('2 applications')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "Join Interview" link if meetLink is provided', () => {
    render(<ApplicationStatus applications={applications} formatDate={formatDateMock} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Join Interview')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "Download CV", "View Passport", and "View Photo" links if the URLs are provided', () => {
    render(<ApplicationStatus applications={applications} formatDate={formatDateMock} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByText('Download CV').length).toBe(2);
    expect(screen.getAllByText('View Passport').length).toBe(2);
    expect(screen.getByText('View Photo')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import DashboardHeader from '../DashboardHeader';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

const mockPush = jest.fn();
jest.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  useRouter: () => ({
    push: mockPush,
  }),
}));

jest.mock('next-themes', () => ({
  useTheme: () => ({
    theme: 'light',
  }),
}));

describe('DashboardHeader', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    mockPush.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the logo', () => {
    render(<DashboardHeader />);
    const logo = screen.getByAltText('International Skills Labor Company');
    expect(logo).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "Back to Home" button and call router.push when clicked', () => {
    render(<DashboardHeader />);
    const backButton = screen.getByText('← Back to Home');
    fireEvent.click(backButton);
    expect(mockPush).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/');
  });

  it('should render the ThemeSwitcher component', () => {
    render(<DashboardHeader />);
    // We can't easily test for the ThemeSwitcher component itself,
    // but we can test for an element that we know is inside it.
    // Or, we can just check if the button to toggle the theme is there.
    // For this case, let's assume the ThemeSwitcher renders a button.
    const themeSwitcher = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Toggle theme/i });
    expect(themeSwitcher).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the contact information in the top bar', () => {
    render(<DashboardHeader />);
    expect(screen.getByText('+358 452644966')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('wassim@internationalskills.fi')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';
import StatusBadge from '../ui/StatusBadge';

interface ApplicationCardProps {
  candidate: Candidate;
  formatDate: (timestamp: any) => string;
}

const ApplicationCard: React.FC<ApplicationCardProps> = ({
  candidate: app,
  formatDate,
}) => {
  return (
    <div key={app.id} className="border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl p-6 hover:shadow-md dark:hover:border-gray-600 transition-shadow">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between gap-6">
        <div className="flex-grow space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              {app.jobTitleText || 'Job Application'}
            </h3>
            <StatusBadge status={app.status} />
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            <p><strong>Category:</strong> {app.jobCategory || 'N/A'}</p>
            {app.specialty && <p><strong>Specialty:</strong> {app.specialty}</p>}
            <p><strong>Submitted:</strong> {formatDate(app.submittedAt)}</p>
            {app.score !== null && app.score !== undefined && (
              <p><strong>Score:</strong> <span className="font-medium">{app.score}/100</span></p>
            )}
            {app.interviewDate && (
              <p><strong>Interview:</strong> {formatDate(app.interviewDate)}</p>
            )}
            {app.meetLink && (
              <p className="md:col-span-2">
                <strong>Meeting Link:</strong>{' '}
                <a
                  href={app.meetLink}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-800 dark:hover:text-blue-300 underline"
                >
                  Join Interview
                </a>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2 flex-shrink-0">
          {app.cvUrl && (
            <a
              href={app.cvUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-300 bg-[#003A70]/10 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#003A70]/20 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 10v6m0 0l-3-3m3 3l3-3m2 8H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" /></svg>
              <span>Download CV</span>
            </a>
          )}
          
          {app.passportUrl && (
            <a
              href={app.passportUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-green-700 dark:text-green-300 bg-green-100 dark:bg-green-900/50 rounded-lg hover:bg-green-200 dark:hover:bg-green-900/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M10 6H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v9a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V8a2 2 0 00-2-2h-5m-4 0V4a2 2 0 114 0v2m-4 0a2 2 0 104 0m-5 8a2 2 0 100-4 2 2 0 000 4zm0 0c1.306 0 2.417.835 2.83 2M9 14a3.001 3.001 0 00-2.83 2M15 11h3m-3 4h2" /></svg>
              <span>View Passport</span>
            </a>
          )}

          {app.photoUrl && (
            <a
              href={app.photoUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 bg-purple-100 dark:bg-purple-900/50 rounded-lg hover:bg-purple-200 dark:hover:bg-purple-900/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z" /></svg>
              <span>View Photo</span>
            </a>
          )}

FILE: app/components/candidate-dashboard/ApplicationStatus.tsx
============================================================
// src/app/components/candidate-dashboard/ApplicationStatus.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';
import ApplicationCard from './ApplicationCard';

interface ApplicationStatusProps {
  applications: Candidate[];
  formatDate: (timestamp: any) => string;
}

const ApplicationStatus: React.FC<ApplicationStatusProps> = ({ applications, formatDate }) => (
  <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none border border-slate-100 dark:border-gray-700">
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Your Applications</h2>
        <span className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          {applications.length} {applications.length === 1 ? 'application' : 'applications'}
        </span>
      </div>

      {applications.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 space-y-4">
          <svg className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" />
          </svg>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">No Applications Yet</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            You haven't submitted any applications yet. Click the button above to get started!
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {applications.map((app) => (
            <ApplicationCard key={app.id} candidate={app} formatDate={formatDate} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  </div>
);

export default ApplicationStatus;

FILE: app/components/candidate-dashboard/InterviewDetails.tsx
============================================================
// src/app/components/candidate-dashboard/InterviewDetails.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface InterviewDetailsProps {
  applications: Candidate[];
  formatDate: (timestamp: any) => string;
}

const InterviewDetails: React.FC<InterviewDetailsProps> = ({ applications, formatDate }) => {
  const scheduledInterviews = applications.filter(app => app.status === 'interview_scheduled');

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none border border-slate-100 dark:border-gray-700">
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-6">Upcoming Interviews</h2>
      {scheduledInterviews.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 space-y-4">
          <svg className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 7V3a1 1 0 011-1h6a1 1 0 011 1v4m-3 8v4m0 0l-3-3m3 3l3-3m-3-4a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z" />
          </svg>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">No Upcoming Interviews</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            You have no interviews scheduled at this time.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {scheduledInterviews.map(app => (
            <div key={app.id} className="border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-6">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{app.jobTitleText}</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400"><strong>Date:</strong> {formatDate(app.interviewDate)}</p>
              <a href={app.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline">Join Interview</a>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default InterviewDetails;

FILE: app/components/candidate-form/__tests__/AdditionalInfoSection.test.tsx
============================================================
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import AdditionalInfoSection from '../AdditionalInfoSection';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('AdditionalInfoSection', () => {
  const addAdditionalInfoMock = jest.fn();
  const removeAdditionalInfoMock = jest.fn();
  const updateAdditionalInfoMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    additionalInfo: [
      { type: 'link' as const, content: 'http://example.com' },
      { type: 'text' as const, content: 'Some text' },
    ],
    addAdditionalInfo: addAdditionalInfoMock,
    removeAdditionalInfo: removeAdditionalInfoMock,
    updateAdditionalInfo: updateAdditionalInfoMock,
    isLoading: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    addAdditionalInfoMock.mockClear();
    removeAdditionalInfoMock.mockClear();
    updateAdditionalInfoMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the "Add Link", "Add File", and "Add Text" buttons', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Add Link')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Add File')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Add Text')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call addAdditionalInfo with the correct type when each button is clicked', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Add Link'));
    expect(addAdditionalInfoMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('link');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Add File'));
    expect(addAdditionalInfoMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('file');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Add Text'));
    expect(addAdditionalInfoMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('text');
  });

  it('should render the correct input field based on the type of the additional info', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('http://example.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('Some text')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call updateAdditionalInfo when the input value is changed', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByDisplayValue('http://example.com'), { target: { value: 'http://new.com' } });
    expect(updateAdditionalInfoMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(0, 'http://new.com');
  });

  it('should call removeAdditionalInfo when the remove button is clicked', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    const removeButtons = screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: '' });
    fireEvent.click(removeButtons[0]);
    expect(removeAdditionalInfoMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(0);
  });

  it('should not show the remove button if there is only one item', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} additionalInfo={[{ type: 'link', content: '' }]} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: '' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should disable the form fields and buttons when isLoading is true', () => {
    render(<AdditionalInfoSection {...defaultProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('http://example.com')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('Some text')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Add Link').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Add File').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Add Text').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

    firstName: 'Test',
    lastName: 'User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    gender: 'male',
    countryCode: '+1',
    phoneNumber: '1234567890',
    dateOfBirth: '1990-01-01',
    city: 'Test City',
    country: 'Test Country',
    nationality: 'Test Nationality',
    selectedCategory: 'IT',
    jobTitle: 'Software Engineer',
    specialty: 'Frontend',
    availableSpecialties: ['Frontend', 'Backend'],
    cvFile: new File(['cv'], 'cv.pdf', { type: 'application/pdf' }),
    passportFile: new File(['passport'], 'passport.pdf', { type: 'application/pdf' }),
    photoFile: null,
    calculateAge: () => 33,
  };

  it('should not render in a production environment', () => {
    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';
    const { container } = render(<DebugInfo {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test'; // Reset for other tests
  });

  it('should render the debug information in a development environment', () => {
    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'development';
    render(<DebugInfo {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Debug Information:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/First Name:/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/"Test"/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test'; // Reset for other tests
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import JobApplicationSection from '../JobApplicationSection';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('JobApplicationSection', () => {
  const handleCategorySelectionMock = jest.fn();
  const handleJobSelectionMock = jest.fn();
  const setSpecialtyMock = jest.fn();
  const setSpecialtySearchMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    selectedCategory: 'Development & IT',
    handleCategorySelection: handleCategorySelectionMock,
    jobTitle: 'job1',
    handleJobSelection: handleJobSelectionMock,
    filteredJobs: [{ id: 'job1', title: 'Software Engineer', description: 'Job description', category: 'Development & IT' }],
    selectedJob: { id: 'job1', title: 'Software Engineer', description: 'Job description', category: 'Development & IT' },
    specialty: 'Frontend',
    setSpecialty: setSpecialtyMock,
    specialtySearch: 'Frontend',
    setSpecialtySearch: setSpecialtySearchMock,
    availableSpecialties: ['Frontend', 'Backend'],
    isLoading: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    handleCategorySelectionMock.mockClear();
    handleJobSelectionMock.mockClear();
    setSpecialtyMock.mockClear();
    setSpecialtySearchMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the form with the correct initial values', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Job Category/)).toHaveValue('Development & IT');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Job Position/)).toHaveValue('job1');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Specialty/)).toHaveValue('Frontend');
  });

  it('should call handleCategorySelection when the job category is changed', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Job Category/), { target: { value: 'Finance' } });
    expect(handleCategorySelectionMock).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('should call handleJobSelection when the job position is changed', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Job Position/), { target: { value: 'job1' } });
    expect(handleJobSelectionMock).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('should call setSpecialty and setSpecialtySearch when the specialty is changed', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Specialty/), { target: { value: 'Backend' } });
    expect(setSpecialtySearchMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Backend');
  });

  it('should show the job description when a job is selected', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Job description')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should show the available specialties when a job is selected', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Available Specialties:')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Frontend')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Backend')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should show a message when no specialties are available', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} availableSpecialties={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No specialties defined for this job position. Please contact admin.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should disable the form fields when isLoading is true', () => {
    render(<JobApplicationSection {...defaultProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Job Category/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Job Position/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Specialty/)).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import PersonalInfoSection from '../PersonalInfoSection';
