jest.mock('../../ui/SearchableDropdown', () => ({
    __esModule: true,
    default: ({ value, onChange, placeholder, disabled }: any) => (
        <input
            data-testid={placeholder}
            value={value}
            onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={placeholder}
            disabled={disabled}
        />
    ),
}));

describe('PersonalInfoSection', () => {
  const setFirstNameMock = jest.fn();
  const setLastNameMock = jest.fn();
  const setGenderMock = jest.fn();
  const setDateOfBirthMock = jest.fn();
  const setCountryCodeMock = jest.fn();
  const setPhoneNumberMock = jest.fn();
  const setCityMock = jest.fn();
  const setCountryMock = jest.fn();
  const setNationalityMock = jest.fn();
  const setDoubleNationalityMock = jest.fn();
  const calculateAgeMock = jest.fn(() => 33);

  const defaultProps = {
    firstName: 'Test',
    setFirstName: setFirstNameMock,
    lastName: 'User',
    setLastName: setLastNameMock,
    email: 'test@example.com',
    gender: 'male',
    setGender: setGenderMock,
    dateOfBirth: '1990-01-01',
    setDateOfBirth: setDateOfBirthMock,
    countryCode: '+1',
    setCountryCode: setCountryCodeMock,
    phoneNumber: '1234567890',
    setPhoneNumber: setPhoneNumberMock,
    city: 'Test City',
    setCity: setCityMock,
    country: 'Test Country',
    setCountry: setCountryMock,
    nationality: 'Test Nationality',
    setNationality: setNationalityMock,
    doubleNationality: 'Test Second Nationality',
    setDoubleNationality: setDoubleNationalityMock,
    isLoading: false,
    isExistingCandidate: false,
    calculateAge: calculateAgeMock,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('should render the form with the correct initial values', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/First Name/)).toHaveValue('Test');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Last Name/)).toHaveValue('User');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Email Address/)).toHaveValue('test@example.com');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Gender/)).toHaveValue('male');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Date of Birth/)).toHaveValue('1990-01-01');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Phone Number/)).toHaveValue('1234567890');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/City/)).toHaveValue('Test City');
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select your country')).toHaveValue('Test Country');
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select your nationality')).toHaveValue('Test Nationality');
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select second nationality')).toHaveValue('Test Second Nationality');
  });

  it('should call the setter functions when the input values are changed', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/First Name/), { target: { value: 'New' } });
    expect(setFirstNameMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('New');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Last Name/), { target: { value: 'Name' } });
    expect(setLastNameMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Name');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Gender/), { target: { value: 'female' } });
    expect(setGenderMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('female');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Date of Birth/), { target: { value: '1991-01-01' } });
    expect(setDateOfBirthMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1991-01-01');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Phone Number/), { target: { value: '0987654321' } });
    expect(setPhoneNumberMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('0987654321');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/City/), { target: { value: 'New City' } });
    expect(setCityMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('New City');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select your country'), { target: { value: 'New Country' } });
    expect(setCountryMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('New Country');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select your nationality'), { target: { value: 'New Nationality' } });
    expect(setNationalityMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('New Nationality');
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select second nationality'), { target: { value: 'New Second Nationality' } });
    expect(setDoubleNationalityMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('New Second Nationality');
  });

  it('should disable the form fields when isLoading is true', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoSection {...defaultProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/First Name/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Last Name/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Gender/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Date of Birth/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Phone Number/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/City/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select your country')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select your nationality')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search and select second nationality')).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('should disable the name, gender, and date of birth fields when isExistingCandidate is true', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoSection {...defaultProps} isExistingCandidate={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/First Name/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Last Name/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Gender/)).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Date of Birth/)).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('should show the age requirement notice', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Age Requirement')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should show the calculated age and validation message', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoSection {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Age: 33 years old/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/candidate-form/containers/CandidateFormUI.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import { ThemeSwitcher } from '../../ui/ThemeSwitcher';
import TopBar from '../../common/TopBar';
import Footer from '../../common/Footer';
import PersonalInfoSection from '../PersonalInfoSection';
import JobApplicationSection from '../JobApplicationSection';
import FileUploadSection from '../FileUploadSection';
import AdditionalInfoSection from '../AdditionalInfoSection';
import SuccessToast from '../../ui/SuccessToast';
import DebugInfo from '../DebugInfo';
import { CandidateFormUIProps } from './types';

const CandidateFormUI: React.FC<CandidateFormUIProps> = ({
    firstName, setFirstName,
    lastName, setLastName,
    email,
    gender, setGender,
    phoneValidation,
    countryCode, setCountryCode,
    phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber,
    dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth,
    city, setCity,
    country, setCountry,
    nationality, setNationality,
    doubleNationality, setDoubleNationality,
    jobTitle,
    specialty, setSpecialty,
    specialtySearch, setSpecialtySearch,
    websites, setWebsites,
    additionalInfo, setAdditionalInfo,
    cvFile,
    passportFile,
    photoFile,
    isLoading,
    error,
    success,
    showSuccessToast, setShowSuccessToast,
    isExistingCandidate,
    addAdditionalInfo,
    removeAdditionalInfo,
    updateAdditionalInfo,
    handleCategorySelection,
    handleJobSelection,
    handleFileChange,
    handleSubmit,
    selectedCategory,
    filteredJobs,
    selectedJob,
    availableSpecialties,
    calculateAge,
}) => {
    const { theme } = useTheme();

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-900 font-sans">
            <header className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 shadow-sm sticky top-0 z-20">
                <TopBar />
                <nav className="max-w-screen-xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-4">
                        <Image
                            src={theme === 'dark' ? "/logo-skills-white.png" : "/logo-skills.png"}
                            alt="International Skills Labor Company"
                            width={180}
                            height={45}
                            priority
                        />
                        <ThemeSwitcher />
                    </div>
                </nav>
            </header>

            <main id="form-start" className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 px-4 sm:px-6">
                <div className="w-full max-w-4xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-8 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shadow-2xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none">
                    <div className="text-center">
                        <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 mb-2">
                            {isExistingCandidate ? 'Submit New Application' : 'Candidate Application'}
                        </h1>
                        <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            {isExistingCandidate ? 'Submit a new job application' : 'Apply for your dream job opportunity'}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-sm text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 mt-2">Application for: <strong>{email}</strong></p>


                        {isExistingCandidate && (
                            <div className="bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30 rounded-lg p-4 mt-4">
                                <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                    <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                                    </svg>
                                    <div className="flex-1">
                                        <p className="text-sm text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 mb-2">
                                            <strong>Welcome back!</strong> We've pre-filled your information.
                                        </p>
                                        <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 space-y-1">
                                            <p><strong>🔒 Locked fields:</strong> Name, gender, and date of birth cannot be modified</p>
                                            <p><strong>✏️ Editable fields:</strong> Phone number and other contact information can be updated</p>
                                            <p><strong>📞 Phone validation:</strong> Skipped for existing candidates (your number is already verified)</p>
                                            <p><strong>📁 Files:</strong> Please upload fresh copies of your documents</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-8">
                        <PersonalInfoSection
                            firstName={firstName} setFirstName={setFirstName}
                            lastName={lastName} setLastName={setLastName}
                            email={email}
                            gender={gender} setGender={setGender}
                            dateOfBirth={dateOfBirth} setDateOfBirth={setDateOfBirth}
                            countryCode={countryCode} setCountryCode={setCountryCode}
                            phoneNumber={phoneNumber} setPhoneNumber={setPhoneNumber}
                            city={city} setCity={setCity}
                            country={country} setCountry={setCountry}
                            nationality={nationality} setNationality={setNationality}
                            doubleNationality={doubleNationality} setDoubleNationality={setDoubleNationality}
                            isLoading={isLoading}
                            isExistingCandidate={isExistingCandidate}
                            calculateAge={calculateAge}
                            phoneValidation={phoneValidation}
                        />

                        <JobApplicationSection
                            selectedCategory={selectedCategory}
                            handleCategorySelection={handleCategorySelection}
                            jobTitle={jobTitle}
                            handleJobSelection={handleJobSelection}
                            filteredJobs={filteredJobs}
                            selectedJob={selectedJob}
                            specialty={specialty}
                            setSpecialty={setSpecialty}
                            specialtySearch={specialtySearch}
                            setSpecialtySearch={setSpecialtySearch}
                            availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
                            isLoading={isLoading}
                        />

                        <AdditionalInfoSection
                            additionalInfo={additionalInfo}
                            addAdditionalInfo={addAdditionalInfo}
                            removeAdditionalInfo={removeAdditionalInfo}
                            updateAdditionalInfo={updateAdditionalInfo}
                            isLoading={isLoading}
                        />

                        <FileUploadSection
                            handleFileChange={handleFileChange}
                            isLoading={isLoading}
                            isExistingCandidate={isExistingCandidate}
                        />

                        <div className="text-center">
                            {error && (
                                <div className="bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
                                    <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-300">{error}</p>
                                </div>
                            )}
                            <DebugInfo
                                firstName={firstName}
                                lastName={lastName}
                                email={email}
                                gender={gender}
                                countryCode={countryCode}
                                phoneNumber={phoneNumber}
                                dateOfBirth={dateOfBirth}
                                city={city}
                                country={country}
                                nationality={nationality}
                                selectedCategory={selectedCategory}
                                jobTitle={jobTitle}
                                specialty={specialty}
                                availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
                                cvFile={cvFile}
                                passportFile={passportFile}
                                photoFile={photoFile}
                                calculateAge={calculateAge}
                            />

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                className="w-full sm:w-auto px-12 py-4 bg-[#003A70] text-white font-bold text-lg tracking-wide rounded-xl shadow-lg hover:bg-[#002B54] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-[#003A70] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all transform hover:scale-[1.02]"
                                disabled={isLoading}
                            >
                                {isLoading ? (
                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2">
                                        <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                                        <span>Submitting...</span>
                                    </div>
                                ) : (
                                    `${isExistingCandidate ? 'Submit New Application' : 'Submit Application'}`
                                )}
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </main>

            <SuccessToast
                message={success || ''}
                isVisible={showSuccessToast}
                onClose={() => setShowSuccessToast(false)}
            />

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
};

export default CandidateFormUI;

FILE: app/components/candidate-form/AdditionalInfoItem.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';

interface AdditionalInfoItemProps {
  info: { type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text'; content: string; file?: File };
  index: number;
  updateAdditionalInfo: (index: number, content: string, file?: File) => void;
  removeAdditionalInfo: (index: number) => void;
  isLoading: boolean;
  canRemove: boolean; // New prop to control remove button visibility
}

const AdditionalInfoItem: React.FC<AdditionalInfoItemProps> = ({
  info,
  index,
  updateAdditionalInfo,
  removeAdditionalInfo,
  isLoading,
  canRemove,
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-2">
      <select
        value={info.type}
        onChange={(e) => {
          updateAdditionalInfo(index, '', undefined); // Clear content and file on type change
          // The parent component will handle updating the type in its state
        }}
        className="px-3 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white min-w-[100px]"
        disabled={isLoading}
      >
        <option value="link">Link</option>
        <option value="file">File</option>
        <option value="text">Text</option>
      </select>
      
      <div className="flex-1 w-full">
        {info.type === 'link' && (
          <input type="url" value={info.content} onChange={(e) => updateAdditionalInfo(index, e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            placeholder="https://linkedin.com/in/yourprofile" disabled={isLoading} />
        )}
        {info.type === 'file' && (
          <input type="file" onChange={(e) => { const file = e.target.files?.[0]; if (file) { updateAdditionalInfo(index, file.name, file); } }}
            className="w-full text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 file:mr-4 file:py-2.5 file:px-4 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:text-sm file:font-semibold file:bg-slate-100 dark:file:bg-gray-600 file:text-[#003A70] dark:file:text-blue-300 hover:file:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:file:bg-gray-500"
            disabled={isLoading} />
        )}
        {info.type === 'text' && (
          <textarea value={info.content} onChange={(e) => updateAdditionalInfo(index, e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            placeholder="Enter additional notes..." rows={2} disabled={isLoading} />
        )}
      </div>
      
      {canRemove && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => removeAdditionalInfo(index)}
          className="p-3 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/50 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-200 dark:hover:bg-red-900/80 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500 self-center sm:self-auto"
          disabled={isLoading}>
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" /></svg>
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default AdditionalInfoItem;

"use client";

import React from 'react';

interface DebugInfoProps {
    firstName: string;
    lastName: string;
    email: string;
    gender: string;
    countryCode: string;
    phoneNumber: string;
    dateOfBirth: string;
    city: string;
    country: string;
    nationality: string;
    selectedCategory: string;
    jobTitle: string;
    specialty: string;
    availableSpecialties: string[];
    cvFile: File | null;
    passportFile: File | null;
    photoFile: File | null;
    calculateAge: (dob: string) => number | null;
}

const DebugInfo: React.FC<DebugInfoProps> = ({
    firstName,
    lastName,
    email,
    gender,
    countryCode,
    phoneNumber,
    dateOfBirth,
    city,
    country,
    nationality,
    selectedCategory,
    jobTitle,
    specialty,
    availableSpecialties,
    cvFile,
    passportFile,
    photoFile,
    calculateAge,
}) => {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'development') {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-100 p-4 rounded-lg mb-6">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-2">Debug Information:</h3>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 space-y-1">
                <p>First Name: "{firstName}" {firstName ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Last Name: "{lastName}" {lastName ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Email: "{email}" {email ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Gender: "{gender}" {gender ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Country Code: "{countryCode}" {countryCode ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Phone: "{phoneNumber}" {phoneNumber ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Date of Birth: "{dateOfBirth}" {dateOfBirth ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>City: "{city}" {city ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Country: "{country}" {country ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Nationality: "{nationality}" {nationality ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Category: "{selectedCategory}" {selectedCategory ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Job Title: "{jobTitle}" {jobTitle ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Specialty: "{specialty}" {specialty ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>Available Specialties: [{availableSpecialties.join(', ')}]</p>
                <p>Specialty Valid: {availableSpecialties.includes(specialty) ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                <p>CV File: {cvFile ? `${cvFile.name} ✅` : 'Missing ❌'}</p>
                <p>Passport File: {passportFile ? `${passportFile.name} ✅` : 'Missing ❌'}</p>
                <p>Photo File: {photoFile ? `${photoFile.name} ✅` : 'Not provided'}</p>
                <p>Age Valid: {dateOfBirth && (calculateAge(dateOfBirth) ?? 0) >= 18 ? '✅' : '❌'}</p>
                {dateOfBirth && (
                    <p>Calculated Age: {calculateAge(dateOfBirth)} years</p>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default DebugInfo;

import React from 'react';

import { JobApplicationSectionProps } from '@/types';
import SpecialtyDropdown from './SpecialtyDropdown';

const JobApplicationSection: React.FC<JobApplicationSectionProps> = ({
  selectedCategory,
  handleCategorySelection,
  jobTitle,
  handleJobSelection,
  filteredJobs,
  selectedJob,
  specialty,
  setSpecialty,
  specialtySearch,
  setSpecialtySearch,
  availableSpecialties,
  isLoading,
}) => {

            name="jobCategory"
            id="jobCategory"
            required

            Job Position <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            name="jobTitle"
            id="jobTitle"
            required
            value={jobTitle}
            onChange={handleJobSelection}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            disabled={isLoading || !selectedCategory}
          >
            <option value="">
              {selectedCategory ? 'Select a job position' : 'Please select a category first'}
            </option>
            {filteredJobs.map((job) => (
              <option key={job.id} value={job.id}>
                {job.title}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {selectedJob && (
            <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-gray-700/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg">
              <h4 className="font-semibold text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 mb-1">Job Description:</h4>

            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Specialty */}
        <SpecialtyDropdown
          specialty={specialty}
          setSpecialty={setSpecialty}
          specialtySearch={specialtySearch}
          setSpecialtySearch={setSpecialtySearch}
          availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
          isLoading={isLoading}
          selectedJob={selectedJob}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: app/components/candidate-form/PersonalInfoSection.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { countryCodes, countries } from '@/lib/formData';
import { PersonalInfoSectionProps } from '@/types';
import SearchableDropdown from '../ui/SearchableDropdown';

interface PersonalInfoSectionPropsExtended extends PersonalInfoSectionProps {
  phoneValidation?: {
    isValid: boolean | null;
    isValidating: boolean;
    message: string;
    warning?: string;
  };
}

const PersonalInfoSection: React.FC<PersonalInfoSectionPropsExtended> = ({
  firstName,
  setFirstName,
  lastName,
  setLastName,
  email,
  gender,
  setGender,
  dateOfBirth,
  setDateOfBirth,
  countryCode,
  setCountryCode,
  phoneNumber,
  setPhoneNumber,
  city,
  setCity,
  country,
  setCountry,
  nationality,
  setNationality,
  doubleNationality,
  setDoubleNationality,
  isLoading,
  isExistingCandidate,
  calculateAge,
  phoneValidation
}) => {
  const countryCodeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showCountryCodeList, setShowCountryCodeList] = useState(false);
  const [countryCodeSearch, setCountryCodeSearch] = useState('');
  
  // 🔥 NEW: Name validation states
  const [firstNameError, setFirstNameError] = useState('');
  const [lastNameError, setLastNameError] = useState('');

  // 🔥 NEW: Name validation function
  const validateName = (name: string, fieldName: string): string => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      return `${fieldName} is required`;
    }
    
    // Check for numbers
    if (/\d/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain numbers`;
    }
    
    // Check for special characters (allow only letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes)
    if (!/^[a-zA-ZÀ-ÿĀ-žА-я\s'-]+$/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} can only contain letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes`;
    }
    
    // Check for dots
    if (/\./.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain dots`;
    }
    
    // Check minimum length
    if (name.trim().length < 2) {
      return `${fieldName} must be at least 2 characters long`;
    }
    
    // Check maximum length
    if (name.trim().length > 50) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot exceed 50 characters`;
    }
    
    // Check for excessive spaces
    if (/\s{2,}/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain multiple consecutive spaces`;
    }
    
    // Check if starts or ends with space/hyphen/apostrophe
    if (/^[\s'-]|[\s'-]$/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot start or end with spaces, hyphens, or apostrophes`;
    }
    
    return '';
  };

  // 🔥 NEW: Handle first name change with validation
  const handleFirstNameChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const value = e.target.value;
    
    // Allow typing but validate after
    setFirstName(value);
    
    // Real-time validation
    const error = validateName(value, 'First name');
    setFirstNameError(error);
  };

  // 🔥 NEW: Handle last name change with validation
  const handleLastNameChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const value = e.target.value;
    
    // Allow typing but validate after
    setLastName(value);
    
    // Real-time validation
    const error = validateName(value, 'Last name');
    setLastNameError(error);
  };

  // 🔥 NEW: Handle input key press (prevent certain characters)
  const handleNameKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const char = e.key;
    
    // Prevent numbers and common special characters from being typed
    if (/[0-9!@#$%^&*()_+=\[\]{};:"\\|,.<>?/~`]/.test(char)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
  };

  // 🔥 NEW: Handle paste event to prevent pasting invalid content
  const handleNamePaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>, fieldName: string) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    const pastedText = e.clipboardData.getData('text');
    const cleanedText = pastedText.replace(/[^a-zA-ZÀ-ÿĀ-žА-я\s'-]/g, '');
    
    if (fieldName === 'firstName') {
      setFirstName(cleanedText);
      const error = validateName(cleanedText, 'First name');
      setFirstNameError(error);
    } else {
      setLastName(cleanedText);
      const error = validateName(cleanedText, 'Last name');
      setLastNameError(error);
    }
  };

  const filteredCountryCodes = countryCodes.filter(item => 
    item.country.toLowerCase().includes(countryCodeSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
    item.code.includes(countryCodeSearch) ||
    item.flag.includes(countryCodeSearch)
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (countryCodeRef.current && !countryCodeRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowCountryCodeList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-lg">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-6 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 pb-3">Personal Information</h2>
      
      {/* Age requirement notice */}
      <div className="bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
          <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
          </svg>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300 mb-1">Age Requirement</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300/80">
              You must be at least <strong>18 years old</strong> to submit an application. 
              Please ensure your date of birth is accurate as it will be verified.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* 🔥 UPDATED: First Name with validation */}
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="firstName" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            First Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            name="firstName"
            id="firstName"
            required
            value={firstName}
            onChange={handleFirstNameChange}
            onKeyPress={handleNameKeyPress}
            onPaste={(e) => handleNamePaste(e, 'firstName')}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-all duration-300 ${
              isExistingCandidate 
                ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600' 
                : firstNameError
                ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 ring-1 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-500/30'
                : firstName && !firstNameError
                ? 'border-green-400 dark:border-green-500 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 ring-1 ring-green-200 dark:ring-green-500/30'
                : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600'
            }`}
            placeholder="Enter your first name"
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
            autoComplete="given-name"
            maxLength={50}
          />
          
          {/* 🔥 NEW: First name validation feedback */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && firstNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">{firstNameError}</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && firstName && !firstNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Valid first name</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && !firstName && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
              Only letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes allowed
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 🔥 UPDATED: Last Name with validation */}
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="lastName" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Last Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            name="lastName"
            id="lastName"
            required
            value={lastName}
            onChange={handleLastNameChange}
            onKeyPress={handleNameKeyPress}
            onPaste={(e) => handleNamePaste(e, 'lastName')}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-all duration-300 ${
              isExistingCandidate 
                ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600' 
                : lastNameError
                ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 ring-1 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-500/30'
                : lastName && !lastNameError
                ? 'border-green-400 dark:border-green-500 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 ring-1 ring-green-200 dark:ring-green-500/30'
                : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600'
            }`}
            placeholder="Enter your last name"
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
            autoComplete="family-name"
            maxLength={50}
          />
          
          {/* 🔥 NEW: Last name validation feedback */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && lastNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">{lastNameError}</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && lastName && !lastNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Valid last name</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && !lastName && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
              Only letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes allowed
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Email Address <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <input
            type="email"
            name="email"
            id="email"
            required
            value={email}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed"
            disabled={true}
          />
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">This email was verified in the previous step</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="gender" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Gender <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <select
            name="gender"
            id="gender"
            required
            value={gender}
            onChange={(e) => setGender(e.target.value)}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white ${
              isExistingCandidate ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed' : ''
            }`}
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
          >
            <option value="">Select Gender</option>
            <option value="male">Male</option>
            <option value="female">Female</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="dateOfBirth" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Date of Birth <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <input
            type="date"
            name="dateOfBirth"
            id="dateOfBirth"
            required
            value={dateOfBirth}
            onChange={(e) => setDateOfBirth(e.target.value)}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white ${
              isExistingCandidate ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed' : ''
            }`}
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
            max={new Date(new Date().setFullYear(new Date().getFullYear() - 18)).toISOString().split('T')[0]}
          />
          {/* Show calculated age with validation */}
          {dateOfBirth && (
            <div className="mt-1">
              {(() => {
                const age = calculateAge ? calculateAge(dateOfBirth) : 0;
                const isValidAge = age !== null && age >= 18;
                
                return (
                  <p className={`text-xs mt-1 ${isValidAge ? 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400' : 'text-red-600 font-medium'}`}>
                    {age !== null && age > 0 ? (
                      isValidAge ? (
                        <>Age: {age} years old</>
                      ) : (
                        <>⚠️ Age: {age} years old - You must be at least 18 years old to apply</>
                      )
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-red-600">Invalid date format</span>
                    )}
                  </p>
                );
              })()}
            </div>
          )}
          {isExistingCandidate && dateOfBirth && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
              Age: {calculateAge ? calculateAge(dateOfBirth) : 0} years old
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 🔥 FIXED: Phone Number Section - Completely different for existing vs new candidates */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <label htmlFor="phoneNumber" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Phone Number <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          
          {/* 🔥 CONDITIONAL: Only show validation info for NEW candidates */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && (
            <div className="bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/20 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-500/30 rounded-lg p-3 mb-3">
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
                <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                </svg>
                <div className="text-xs text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300">
                  <p className="font-medium mb-1">📞 Phone Verification System</p>
                  <p>Your phone number will be validated using our secure 3-tier verification system to ensure accuracy and deliverability.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex space-x-2">
            {/* Country Code Dropdown */}
            <div className="relative" ref={countryCodeRef}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (!isLoading && !isExistingCandidate) {
                    setShowCountryCodeList(!showCountryCodeList);
                    setCountryCodeSearch('');
                  }
                }}
                className={`w-32 px-3 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-l-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] text-sm flex items-center justify-between ${
                  isLoading || isExistingCandidate 
                    ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed opacity-75' 
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-gray-700 cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600'
                }`}
                disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
              >
                <span className="dark:text-white">
                  {countryCodes.find(item => item.code === countryCode)?.flag} {countryCode}
                </span>
                {!isExistingCandidate && (
                  <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
                  </svg>
                )}
              </button>
              
              {showCountryCodeList && !isLoading && !isExistingCandidate && (
                <div className="absolute z-20 w-80 mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg">
                  <div className="p-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-600">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={countryCodeSearch}
                      onChange={(e) => setCountryCodeSearch(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-600 dark:text-white"
                      placeholder="Search country..."
                      autoFocus
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
                    {filteredCountryCodes.map((item, index) => (
                      <button
                        key={index}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setCountryCode(item.code);
                          setShowCountryCodeList(false);
                          setCountryCodeSearch('');
                        }}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none flex items-center space-x-3"
                      >
                        <span className="text-lg">{item.flag}</span>
                        <span className="font-medium dark:text-white">{item.code}</span>
                        <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 truncate">{item.country}</span>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                    {filteredCountryCodes.length === 0 && (
                      <div className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        No countries found
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            
            {/* Phone Number Input */}
            <div className="flex-1">
              <input
                type="tel"
                name="phoneNumber"
                id="phoneNumber"
                required
                value={phoneNumber}
                onChange={(e) => setPhoneNumber(e.target.value)}
                className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-r-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-all duration-300 ${
                  isExistingCandidate 
                    ? 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed' 
                    : phoneValidation?.isValid === true 
                    ? 'border-green-400 dark:border-green-500 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 ring-1 ring-green-200 dark:ring-green-500/30' 
                    : phoneValidation?.isValid === false 
                    ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 ring-1 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-500/30'
                    : phoneValidation?.isValidating
                    ? 'border-blue-400 dark:border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 ring-1 ring-blue-200 dark:ring-blue-500/30'
                    : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600'
                }`}
                placeholder="Enter your phone number"
                disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          {/* 🔥 CONDITIONAL: Phone Validation Feedback - ONLY for new candidates */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && (phoneValidation?.isValidating || phoneValidation?.message) && (
            <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg border transition-all duration-300" style={{
              backgroundColor: phoneValidation.isValid === true 
                ? 'rgb(240 253 244)' 
                : phoneValidation.isValid === false 
                ? 'rgb(254 242 242)'
                : 'rgb(239 246 255)',
              borderColor: phoneValidation.isValid === true 
                ? 'rgb(34 197 94)' 
                : phoneValidation.isValid === false 
                ? 'rgb(239 68 68)'
                : 'rgb(59 130 246)'
            }}>
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                {phoneValidation.isValidating && (
                  <div className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                    <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                  </div>
                )}
                
                {!phoneValidation.isValidating && (
                  <div className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                    {phoneValidation.isValid === true ? (
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-green-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : phoneValidation.isValid === false ? (
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-red-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M10 14l2-2m0 0l2-2m-2 2l-2-2m2 2l2 2m7-2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : (
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                      </svg>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
                
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${
                    phoneValidation.isValid === true 
                      ? 'text-green-700' 
                      : phoneValidation.isValid === false 
                      ? 'text-red-700'
                      : 'text-blue-700'
                  }`}>
                    {phoneValidation.message}
                  </p>
                  
                  {phoneValidation.isValidating && (
                    <div className="mt-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs text-blue-600">
                        <span>Verifying through secure validation system...</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="mt-1 w-full bg-blue-200 rounded-full h-1">
                        <div className="bg-blue-600 h-1 rounded-full animate-pulse" style={{ width: '60%' }}></div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  
                  {phoneValidation.warning && (
                    <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
                      <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-2.5L13.732 4c-.77-.833-1.964-.833-2.732 0L3.732 16.5c-.77.833.192 2.5 1.732 2.5z" />
                      </svg>
                      <p className="text-xs text-amber-700 font-medium">
                        {phoneValidation.warning}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* 🔥 CONDITIONAL: Help text based on user type */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && !phoneValidation?.message && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-2">
              Enter your phone number without the country code (e.g., for +358 123 456 789, enter "123 456 789")
            </p>
          )}

          {isExistingCandidate && (
            <div className="mt-2 p-2 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 rounded-lg border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30">
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 font-medium">
                🔒 Phone number from your previous application (already validated).
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="city" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            City <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            name="city"
            id="city"
            required
            value={city}
            onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            placeholder="Enter your city"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="country" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Country <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <SearchableDropdown
            options={countries}
            value={country}
            onChange={setCountry}
            placeholder="Search and select your country"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="nationality" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Nationality <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <SearchableDropdown
            options={countries}
            value={nationality}
            onChange={setNationality}
            placeholder="Search and select your nationality"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="doubleNationality" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Second Nationality <span className="text-slate-400">(Optional)</span>
          </label>
          <SearchableDropdown
            options={countries}
            value={doubleNationality}
            onChange={setDoubleNationality}
            placeholder="Search and select second nationality"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PersonalInfoSection;

FILE: app/components/candidate-form/SpecialtyDropdown.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Job } from '@/types';

interface SpecialtyDropdownProps {
  specialty: string;
  setSpecialty: (specialty: string) => void;
  specialtySearch: string;
  setSpecialtySearch: (search: string) => void;
  availableSpecialties: string[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  selectedJob: Job | null;
}

const SpecialtyDropdown: React.FC<SpecialtyDropdownProps> = ({
  specialty,
  setSpecialty,
  specialtySearch,
  setSpecialtySearch,
  availableSpecialties,
  isLoading,
  selectedJob,
}) => {
  const specialtyRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showSpecialtyList, setShowSpecialtyList] = useState(false);

  const filteredSpecialties = availableSpecialties.filter(spec => 
    spec.toLowerCase().includes(specialtySearch.toLowerCase())
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (specialtyRef.current && !specialtyRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowSpecialtyList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="sm:col-span-2 relative" ref={specialtyRef}>
      <label htmlFor="specialtySearch" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
        Specialty <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
      </label>
      <input
        type="text"
        name="specialtySearch"
        id="specialtySearch"
        required
        value={specialtySearch}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const value = e.target.value;
          setSpecialtySearch(value);
          if (availableSpecialties.includes(value)) {
            setSpecialty(value);
          } else {
            setSpecialty('');
          }
          setShowSpecialtyList(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setShowSpecialtyList(true)}
        className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
        placeholder={selectedJob ? "Search and select your specialty" : "Please select a job position first"}
        disabled={isLoading || !selectedJob}
        autoComplete="off"
      />
      {showSpecialtyList && filteredSpecialties.length > 0 && selectedJob && (
        <div className="absolute z-10 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredSpecialties.map((spec, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSpecialty(spec);
                setSpecialtySearch(spec);
                setShowSpecialtyList(false);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 text-left hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none"
            >
              {spec}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {selectedJob && availableSpecialties.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-2 p-3 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 rounded-lg">
          <p className="text-sm text-green-800 dark:text-green-300 font-medium">Available Specialties:</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-1">
            {availableSpecialties.map((spec, index) => (
              <span key={index} className="px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-green-200 dark:bg-green-900/50 text-green-800 dark:text-green-300 rounded-full">
                {spec}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {selectedJob && availableSpecialties.length === 0 && (
        <div className="mt-2 p-3 bg-yellow-50 dark:bg-yellow-900/20 rounded-lg">
          <p className="text-sm text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300">No specialties defined for this job position. Please contact admin.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SpecialtyDropdown;

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import CandidateTableActions from '../CandidateTableActions';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('CandidateTableActions', () => {
  const onViewCandidateMock = jest.fn();
  const onScoreCandidateMock = jest.fn();
  const onEditCandidateMock = jest.fn();

  const candidate = { id: '1', fullName: 'Test User', email: 'test@test.com', status: 'submitted', submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() } };

  const defaultProps = {
    candidate,
    onViewCandidate: onViewCandidateMock,
    onScoreCandidate: onScoreCandidateMock,
  };

  const renderInTable = (ui: React.ReactElement) => {
    return render(
      <table>
        <tbody>
          <tr>{ui}</tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    );
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onViewCandidateMock.mockClear();
    onScoreCandidateMock.mockClear();
    onEditCandidateMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the "View" and "Score" buttons', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableActions {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('View')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Score')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "Edit" button when onEditCandidate is provided', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableActions {...defaultProps} onEditCandidate={onEditCandidateMock} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Edit')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should not render the "Edit" button when onEditCandidate is not provided', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableActions {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Edit')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onViewCandidate when the "View" button is clicked', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableActions {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('View'));
    expect(onViewCandidateMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(candidate);
  });

  it('should call onScoreCandidate when the "Score" button is clicked', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableActions {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Score'));
    expect(onScoreCandidateMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(candidate);
  });

  it('should call onEditCandidate when the "Edit" button is clicked', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableActions {...defaultProps} onEditCandidate={onEditCandidateMock} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Edit'));
    expect(onEditCandidateMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(candidate);
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import CandidateTableRow from '../CandidateTableRow';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

jest.mock('../CandidateTableActions', () => () => <td data-testid="actions-mock">Actions</td>);

describe('CandidateTableRow', () => {
  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    status: 'submitted',
    dateOfBirth: '1990-01-01',
    city: 'Test City',
    country: 'Test Country',
    score: 85,
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-01-01') },
  };

  const defaultProps = {
    candidate,
    splitName: (name: string) => ({ firstName: name.split(' ')[0], lastName: name.split(' ')[1] }),
    calculateAge: () => 33,
    onViewCandidate: jest.fn(),
    onScoreCandidate: jest.fn(),
    onEditCandidate: jest.fn(),
  };

  const renderInTable = (ui: React.ReactElement) => {
    return render(
      <table>
        <tbody>{ui}</tbody>
      </table>
    );
  };

  it("should render the candidate's full name and email", () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test User')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByText('test@example.com').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("should display the candidate's age if dateOfBirth is provided", () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('33')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should display "N/A" for age if dateOfBirth is not provided', () => {
    const candidateWithoutDob = { ...candidate, dateOfBirth: undefined };
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} candidate={candidateWithoutDob} calculateAge={() => null} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByText('N/A').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("should display the candidate's city and country", () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test City')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Country')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should display the submitted date', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(new Date('2023-01-01').toLocaleDateString())).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should display the AI score', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('85')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should display the status badge', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Application Submitted')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the actions component', () => {
    renderInTable(<CandidateTableRow {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('actions-mock')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import ExportButtons from '../ExportButtons';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ExportButtons', () => {
  const handleExportMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    isExporting: false,
    handleExport: handleExportMock,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    handleExportMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the three export buttons', () => {
    render(<ExportButtons {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Export CSV')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Export XLSX')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Export JSON')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call handleExport with "csv" when the CSV button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ExportButtons {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Export CSV'));
    expect(handleExportMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('csv');
  });

  it('should call handleExport with "xlsx" when the XLSX button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ExportButtons {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Export XLSX'));
    expect(handleExportMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('xlsx');
  });

  it('should call handleExport with "json" when the JSON button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ExportButtons {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Export JSON'));
    expect(handleExportMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('json');
  });

  it('should disable the buttons and show "Exporting..." when isExporting is true', () => {
    render(<ExportButtons {...defaultProps} isExporting={true} />);
    const buttons = screen.getAllByRole('button');
    buttons.forEach(button => {
        expect(button).toBeDisabled();
        expect(button).toHaveTextContent('Exporting...');
    });
  });
});

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface CandidateTableActionsProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onViewCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onScoreCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onEditCandidate?: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
}

interface ActionButtonProps {
    onClick: () => void;
    icon: React.ReactElement;
    srText: string;
    color: string;
}

const ActionButton: React.FC<ActionButtonProps> = ({ onClick, icon, srText, color }) => (
    <button
        onClick={onClick}
        className={`p-2 rounded-full text-${color}-500 hover:bg-${color}-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 hover:text-${color}-700 dark:text-${color}-400 dark:hover:text-${color}-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-${color}-500 transition-colors`}
    >
        <span className="sr-only">{srText}</span>
        {icon}
    </button>
);

const CandidateTableActions: React.FC<CandidateTableActionsProps> = ({
    candidate,
    onViewCandidate,
    onScoreCandidate,
    onEditCandidate,
}) => {
    return (
        <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-right text-sm font-medium">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-1">
                <ActionButton
                    onClick={() => onViewCandidate(candidate)}
                    icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15 12a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z" /><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M2.458 12C3.732 7.943 7.523 5 12 5c4.478 0 8.268 2.943 9.542 7-1.274 4.057-5.064 7-9.542 7-4.477 0-8.268-2.943-9.542-7z" /></svg>}
                    srText="View"
                    color="blue"
                />
                {onEditCandidate && (
                    <ActionButton
                        onClick={() => onEditCandidate(candidate)}
                        icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z" /></svg>}
                        srText="Edit"
                        color="green"
                    />
                )}
                <ActionButton
                    onClick={() => onScoreCandidate(candidate)}
                    icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M11.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.519 4.674a1 1 0 00.95.69h4.915c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-3.976 2.888a1 1 0 00-.363 1.118l1.518 4.674c.3.922-.755 1.688-1.539 1.118l-3.975-2.888a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-3.976 2.888c-.783.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.518-4.674a1 1 0 00-.363-1.118l-3.976-2.888c-.784-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h4.914a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.519-4.674z" /></svg>}
                    srText="Score"
                    color="purple"
                />
            </div>
        </td>
    );
};

export default CandidateTableActions;

FILE: app/components/candidate-table/CandidateTableHeader.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { SortDirection, SortField } from '@/types';

interface CandidateTableHeaderProps {
    sortField: SortField;
    sortDirection: SortDirection;
    handleSort: (field: SortField) => void;
}

const SortableHeader: React.FC<{
    field: SortField;
    label: string;
    sortField: SortField;
    sortDirection: SortDirection;
    handleSort: (field: SortField) => void;
}> = ({ field, label, sortField, sortDirection, handleSort }) => {
    const isSorted = sortField === field;
    
    const getSortIcon = () => {
        if (!isSorted) {
            return (
                <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-300 dark:text-gray-500 group-hover:text-gray-400 dark:group-hover:text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 9l4-4 4 4m0 6l-4 4-4-4" />
                </svg>
            );
        }
        return sortDirection === 'asc' ? (
            <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M5 15l7-7 7 7" />
            </svg>
        ) : (
            <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
            </svg>
        );
    };

    return (
        <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
            <button
                onClick={() => handleSort(field)}
                className="group flex items-center space-x-2"
            >
                <span>{label}</span>
                {getSortIcon()}
            </button>
        </th>
    );
};

const CandidateTableHeader: React.FC<CandidateTableHeaderProps> = ({
    sortField,
    sortDirection,
    handleSort,
}) => {
    const headers: { field: SortField; label: string }[] = [
        { field: 'firstName', label: 'Name' },
        { field: 'email', label: 'Email' },
        { field: 'age', label: 'Age' },
        { field: 'city', label: 'City' },
        { field: 'country', label: 'Country' },
        { field: 'submittedAt', label: 'Submitted' },
        { field: 'status', label: 'Status' },
        { field: 'score', label: 'AI Score' },
    ];

    return (
        <thead className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600">
            <tr>
                {headers.map((header) => (
                    <SortableHeader
                        key={header.field}
                        field={header.field}
                        label={header.label}
                        sortField={sortField}
                        sortDirection={sortDirection}
                        handleSort={handleSort}
                    />
                ))}
                <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Actions
                </th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
    );
};

export default CandidateTableHeader;

FILE: app/components/candidate-table/CandidateTableRow.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import StatusBadge from '../ui/StatusBadge';
import CandidateTableActions from './CandidateTableActions';

interface CandidateTableRowProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    splitName: (fullName: string) => { firstName: string; lastName: string };
    calculateAge: (dateOfBirth: string) => number | null;
    onViewCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onScoreCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onEditCandidate?: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
}

const CandidateTableRow: React.FC<CandidateTableRowProps> = ({
    candidate,
    splitName,
    calculateAge,
    onViewCandidate,
    onScoreCandidate,
    onEditCandidate,
}) => {
    const { firstName, lastName } = splitName(candidate.fullName || '');
    const age = candidate.dateOfBirth ? calculateAge(candidate.dateOfBirth) : null;

    return (
        <tr key={candidate.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-700/50 transition-colors duration-150">
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-medium text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                <div className="flex items-center">
                    <div className="flex-shrink-0 h-10 w-10">
                        {candidate.photoUrl ? (
                            <img className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full object-cover" src={candidate.photoUrl} alt="" />
                        ) : (
                            <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center">
                                <span className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{firstName.charAt(0)}{lastName.charAt(0)}</span>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="ml-4">
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{firstName} {lastName}</div>
                        <div className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 md:hidden">{candidate.email}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.email}
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {age || 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.city || 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.country || 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.submittedAt
                    ? new Date(candidate.submittedAt.toDate()).toLocaleDateString()
                    : 'N/A'
                }
            </td>
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                <StatusBadge status={candidate.status} />
            </td>
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                {candidate.score !== null ? candidate.score : 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <CandidateTableActions
                candidate={candidate}
                onViewCandidate={onViewCandidate}
                onScoreCandidate={onScoreCandidate}
                onEditCandidate={onEditCandidate}
            />
        </tr>
    );
};

export default CandidateTableRow;

import React from 'react';

interface ExportButtonsProps {

}

const ExportButtons: React.FC<ExportButtonsProps> = ({ isExporting, handleExport }) => {
    return (
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
                onClick={() => handleExport('csv')}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-gray-400"
            >
                {isExporting ? 'Exporting...' : 'Export CSV'}
            </button>
            <button
                onClick={() => handleExport('xlsx')}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white bg-green-600 rounded-md hover:bg-green-700 disabled:bg-gray-400"
            >
                {isExporting ? 'Exporting...' : 'Export XLSX'}
            </button>
            <button
                onClick={() => handleExport('json')}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white bg-gray-600 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:bg-gray-400"
            >
                {isExporting ? 'Exporting...' : 'Export JSON'}
            </button>
        </div>
    );
};
