"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface QuickFilterButtonsProps {
  label: string;
  options: string[];
  filterValue: string;
  onFilterChange: (value: string) => void;
  candidates: Candidate[];
  filterKey: keyof Candidate;
  colorClass: string;
  sliceLimit?: number;
}

const QuickFilterButtons: React.FC<QuickFilterButtonsProps> = ({
  label,
  options,
  filterValue,
  onFilterChange,
  candidates,
  filterKey,
  colorClass,
  sliceLimit,
}) => {
  const displayOptions = sliceLimit ? options.slice(0, sliceLimit) : options;

  // 🔥 FIXED: Define static color combinations to ensure Tailwind includes them
  const getColorClasses = (color: string, isSelected: boolean) => {
    const colorMap = {
      blue: {
        selected: 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-300 border-blue-300 dark:border-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/20'
      },
      green: {
        selected: 'bg-green-600 text-white border-green-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-green-600 dark:text-green-300 border-green-300 dark:border-green-600 hover:bg-green-50 dark:hover:bg-green-900/20'
      },
      purple: {
        selected: 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-300 border-purple-300 dark:border-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-50 dark:hover:bg-purple-900/20'
      },
      orange: {
        selected: 'bg-orange-600 text-white border-orange-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-orange-600 dark:text-orange-300 border-orange-300 dark:border-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-900/20'
      },
      red: {
        selected: 'bg-red-600 text-white border-red-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-red-600 dark:text-red-300 border-red-300 dark:border-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-900/20'
      },
      indigo: {
        selected: 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-300 border-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/20'
      },
      pink: {
        selected: 'bg-pink-600 text-white border-pink-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-pink-600 dark:text-pink-300 border-pink-300 dark:border-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-50 dark:hover:bg-pink-900/20'
      },
      teal: {
        selected: 'bg-teal-600 text-white border-teal-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300 border-teal-300 dark:border-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-50 dark:hover:bg-teal-900/20'
      },
      cyan: {
        selected: 'bg-cyan-600 text-white border-cyan-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-300 border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-50 dark:hover:bg-cyan-900/20'
      },
      slate: {
        selected: 'bg-slate-600 text-white border-slate-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-900/20'
      }
    };

    // Fallback to blue if color not found
    const colorConfig = colorMap[color as keyof typeof colorMap] || colorMap.blue;
    return isSelected ? colorConfig.selected : colorConfig.unselected;
  };

  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 mr-3">{label}:</span>
      <div className="inline-flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-1">
        {displayOptions.map(option => {
          const count = candidates.filter(c => c[filterKey] === option).length;
          const isSelected = filterValue === option;
          
          return (
            <button
              key={option}
              onClick={() => {
                if (isSelected) {
                  onFilterChange(''); // Deselect if already selected
                } else {
                  onFilterChange(option);
                }
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-all duration-200 font-medium ${getColorClasses(colorClass, isSelected)}`}
              title={`Filter by ${option} (${count} ${count === 1 ? 'candidate' : 'candidates'})`}
            >
              {option} ({count})
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default QuickFilterButtons;

============================================================
FILE: app/components/common/SearchableFilterDropdown.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface SearchableFilterDropdownProps {
  label: string;
  placeholder: string;
  filterValue: string; // The currently applied filter value from parent state
  onFilterChange: (value: string) => void; // Handler to update parent filter state
  searchValue: string; // Local search input value
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void; // Handler to update local search state
  options: string[]; // All unique options available
  candidates: Candidate[]; // All candidates for counting
  filterKey: keyof Candidate; // Key to filter candidates by (e.g., 'city', 'country')
  disabled?: boolean;
}

const SearchableFilterDropdown: React.FC<SearchableFilterDropdownProps> = ({
  label,
  placeholder,
  filterValue,
  onFilterChange,
  searchValue,
  onSearchChange,
  options,
  candidates,
  filterKey,
  disabled = false,
}) => {
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(false);

  const filteredOptions = options.filter(option => 
    option.toLowerCase().includes(searchValue.toLowerCase())
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={dropdownRef}>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">{label}</label>
      <input
        type="text"
        placeholder={placeholder}
        value={searchValue}
        onChange={(e) => {
          onSearchChange(e.target.value);
          onFilterChange(''); // Clear parent filter when typing in search
          setShowList(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setShowList(true)}
        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
        autoComplete="off"
        disabled={disabled}
      />
      {showList && filteredOptions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute z-20 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredOptions.map((option, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onFilterChange(option);
                onSearchChange(option); // Update local search to match selected option
                setShowList(false);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 text-left hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-gray-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none flex justify-between items-center text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
            >
              <span>{option}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                ({candidates.filter(c => c[filterKey] === option).length})
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SearchableFilterDropdown;

FILE: app/components/edit-candidate-modal/__tests__/EditModalFooter.test.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import EditModalFooter from '../EditModalFooter';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('EditModalFooter', () => {
  const onCloseMock = jest.fn();
  const handleSubmitMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    onClose: onCloseMock,
    handleSubmit: handleSubmitMock,
    isLoading: false,
    loadingJobs: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onCloseMock.mockClear();
    handleSubmitMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the "Cancel" and "Save Changes" buttons', () => {
    render(<EditModalFooter {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Cancel')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Save Changes')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onClose when the "Cancel" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<EditModalFooter {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Cancel'));
    expect(onCloseMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call handleSubmit when the "Save Changes" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<EditModalFooter {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Save Changes'));
    expect(handleSubmitMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should disable the buttons and show "Saving..." when isLoading is true', () => {
    render(<EditModalFooter {...defaultProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Saving...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Cancel')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Saving...').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('should disable the "Save Changes" button when loadingJobs is true', () => {
    render(<EditModalFooter {...defaultProps} loadingJobs={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Save Changes').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import JobInfoForm from '../JobInfoForm';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('JobInfoForm', () => {
  const handleCategoryChangeMock = jest.fn();
  const handleJobChangeMock = jest.fn();
  const handleInputChangeMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    formData: {
      jobCategory: 'IT',
      jobTitle: 'job1',
      specialty: 'Frontend',
      jobTitleText: 'Software Engineer',
    },
    jobCategories: ['IT', 'Finance'],
    filteredJobs: [{ id: 'job1', title: 'Software Engineer' }],
    availableSpecialties: ['Frontend', 'Backend'],
    handleCategoryChange: handleCategoryChangeMock,
    handleJobChange: handleJobChangeMock,
    handleInputChange: handleInputChangeMock,
    isLoading: false,
    loadingJobs: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    handleCategoryChangeMock.mockClear();
    handleJobChangeMock.mockClear();
    handleInputChangeMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the form with the correct initial values', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Job Category')).toHaveValue('IT');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Job Position')).toHaveValue('job1');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Specialty')).toHaveValue('Frontend');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Current Job Title')).toHaveValue('Software Engineer');
  });

  it('should call handleCategoryChange when the job category is changed', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Job Category'), { target: { value: 'Finance' } });
    expect(handleCategoryChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Finance');
  });

  it('should call handleJobChange when the job position is changed', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Job Position'), { target: { value: 'job1' } });
    expect(handleJobChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job1');
  });

  it('should call handleInputChange when the specialty is changed', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Specialty'), { target: { value: 'Backend' } });
    expect(handleInputChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('specialty', 'Backend');
  });

  it('should show a loading indicator when loadingJobs is true', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} loadingJobs={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Loading jobs...')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should disable the form fields when isLoading is true', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Job Category')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Job Position')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Specialty')).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('should show a message when no jobs are available for a category', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} filteredJobs={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No active jobs available in this category')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should show a message when no specialties are available for a job', () => {
    render(<JobInfoForm {...defaultProps} availableSpecialties={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No specialties available for this job')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';

jest.mock('../../ui/SearchableDropdown', () => ({
    __esModule: true,

        <input
            data-testid={placeholder}

FILE: app/components/edit-candidate-modal/EditCandidateForm.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import PersonalInfoForm from './PersonalInfoForm';
import LocationInfoForm from './LocationInfoForm';
import JobInfoForm from './JobInfoForm';

interface EditCandidateFormProps {
    formData: Partial<Candidate>;
    errors: Record<string, string>;
    jobCategories: any[];
    loadingJobs: boolean;
    availableSpecialties: string[];
    filteredJobs: any[];
    handleInputChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleCategoryChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleJobChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
    isLoading: boolean;
}

const EditCandidateForm: React.FC<EditCandidateFormProps> = ({
    formData,
    errors,
    jobCategories,
    loadingJobs,
    availableSpecialties,
    filteredJobs,
    handleInputChange,
    handleCategoryChange,
    handleJobChange,
    handleSubmit,
    isLoading,
}) => {
    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <PersonalInfoForm
                formData={formData}
                errors={errors}
                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                isLoading={isLoading}
            />
            <LocationInfoForm
                formData={formData}
                errors={errors}
                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                isLoading={isLoading}
            />
            <JobInfoForm
                formData={formData}
                jobCategories={jobCategories}
                filteredJobs={filteredJobs}
                availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
                handleCategoryChange={handleCategoryChange}
                handleJobChange={handleJobChange}
                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                isLoading={isLoading}
                loadingJobs={loadingJobs}
            />
        </form>
    );
};

export default EditCandidateForm;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiSave } from 'react-icons/fi';

interface EditModalFooterProps {
    onClose: () => void;
    handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => Promise<void>;
    isLoading: boolean;
    loadingJobs: boolean;
}

const EditModalFooter: React.FC<EditModalFooterProps> = ({ onClose, handleSubmit, isLoading, loadingJobs }) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-6 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-end space-x-3 flex-shrink-0">
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors"
                disabled={isLoading}
            >
                Cancel
            </button>
            <button
                onClick={handleSubmit}
                disabled={isLoading || loadingJobs}
                className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
                {isLoading ? (
                    <>
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mr-2"></div>
                        Saving...
                    </>
                ) : (
                    <>
                        <FiSave className="mr-2" />
                        Save Changes
                    </>
                )}
            </button>
        </div>
    );
};

export default EditModalFooter;

FILE: app/components/edit-candidate-modal/JobInfoForm.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiBriefcase } from 'react-icons/fi';

interface JobInfoFormProps {
    formData: any;
    jobCategories: string[];
    filteredJobs: any[];
    availableSpecialties: string[];
    handleCategoryChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleJobChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleInputChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    isLoading: boolean;
    loadingJobs: boolean;
}

const JobInfoForm: React.FC<JobInfoFormProps> = ({
    formData,
    jobCategories,
    filteredJobs,
    availableSpecialties,
    handleCategoryChange,
    handleJobChange,
    handleInputChange,
    isLoading,
    loadingJobs,
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-4 flex items-center">
                <FiBriefcase className="mr-2" /> Job Information
            </h3>
            {loadingJobs && (
                <div className="text-center py-4">
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                        <span className="text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">Loading jobs...</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="jobCategory" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Job Category
                    </label>
                    <select
                        id="jobCategory"
                        name="jobCategory"
                        value={formData.jobCategory}
                        onChange={handleCategoryChange}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                        disabled={isLoading || loadingJobs}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select Category</option>
                        {jobCategories.map((category) => (
                            <option key={category} value={category}>
                                {category}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="jobTitle" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Job Position
                    </label>
                    <select
                        id="jobTitle"
                        name="jobTitle"
                        value={formData.jobTitle}
                        onChange={handleJobChange}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                        disabled={isLoading || loadingJobs || !formData.jobCategory}
                    >
                        <option value="">
                            {!formData.jobCategory
                                ? 'Select category first'
                                : 'Select job position'
                            }
                        </option>
                        {filteredJobs.map((job) => (
                            <option key={job.id} value={job.id}>
                                {job.title}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                    {formData.jobCategory && filteredJobs.length === 0 && !loadingJobs && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-orange-600 mt-1">
                            No active jobs available in this category
                        </p>
                    )}
                </div>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                    <label htmlFor="specialty" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Specialty
                    </label>
                    <select
                        id="specialty"
                        name="specialty"
                        value={formData.specialty}
                        onChange={handleInputChange}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                        disabled={isLoading || !formData.jobTitle}
                    >
                        <option value="">
                            {!formData.jobTitle
                                ? 'Select job position first'
                                : 'Select specialty'
                            }
                        </option>
                        {availableSpecialties.map((specialty) => (
                            <option key={specialty} value={specialty}>
                                {specialty}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                    {formData.jobTitle && availableSpecialties.length === 0 && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-orange-600 mt-1">
                            No specialties available for this job
                        </p>
                    )}
                </div>
                {formData.jobTitleText && (
                    <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <label htmlFor="jobTitleText" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                            Current Job Title
                        </label>
                        <input
                            id="jobTitleText"
                            name="jobTitleText"
                            type="text"
                            value={formData.jobTitleText}
                            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-md bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400"
                            disabled
                            readOnly
                        />
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
                            This will be updated automatically when you select a job position
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default JobInfoForm;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiMapPin } from 'react-icons/fi';
import SearchableDropdown from '../ui/SearchableDropdown';
import { countries } from '@/lib/formData';

interface LocationInfoFormProps {
    formData: any;
    errors: any;
    handleInputChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    isLoading: boolean;
}

const LocationInfoForm: React.FC<LocationInfoFormProps> = ({ formData, errors, handleInputChange, isLoading }) => {
    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-4 flex items-center">
                <FiMapPin className="mr-2" /> Location Information
            </h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="city" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        City <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <input
                        id="city"
                        name="city"
                        type="text"
                        value={formData.city}
                        onChange={handleInputChange}
                        className={`w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 ${errors.city ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600'} bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200`}
                        disabled={isLoading}
                    />
                    {errors.city && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs mt-1">{errors.city}</p>}
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="country" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Country <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <SearchableDropdown
                        id="country"
                        options={countries}
                        value={formData.country}
                        onChange={(value) => handleInputChange({ target: { name: 'country', value } } as React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>)}
                        placeholder="Search and select country"
                        disabled={isLoading}
                    />
                    {errors.country && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs mt-1">{errors.country}</p>}
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="nationality" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Nationality <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <SearchableDropdown
                        id="nationality"
                        options={countries}
                        value={formData.nationality}
                        onChange={(value) => handleInputChange({ target: { name: 'nationality', value } } as React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>)}
                        placeholder="Search and select nationality"
                        disabled={isLoading}
                    />
                    {errors.nationality && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs mt-1">{errors.nationality}</p>}
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="doubleNationality" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Second Nationality <span className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">(Optional)</span>
                    </label>
                    <SearchableDropdown
                        id="doubleNationality"
                        options={countries}
                        value={formData.doubleNationality}
                        onChange={(value) => handleInputChange({ target: { name: 'doubleNationality', value } } as React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>)}
                        placeholder="Search and select second nationality"
                        disabled={isLoading}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default LocationInfoForm;

                        disabled={isLoading}

                            placeholder="123 456 789"

                        />
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );

FILE: app/components/profile-modal/__tests__/ActionsCard.test.tsx
============================================================

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import ActionsCard from '../ActionsCard';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ActionsCard', () => {
  const onStatusChangeMock = jest.fn();
  const onScheduleInterviewMock = jest.fn();

  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    status: 'submitted',
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() },
  };

  const defaultProps = {
    candidate,
    onStatusChange: onStatusChangeMock,
    onScheduleInterview: onScheduleInterviewMock,
    statusUpdateLoading: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onStatusChangeMock.mockClear();
    onScheduleInterviewMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the "Schedule Interview", "Approve", and "Reject" buttons when the candidate status is "submitted"', () => {
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Schedule Interview')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Approve')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Reject')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "Approve" and "Reject" buttons when the candidate status is "interview_scheduled"', () => {
    const interviewScheduledCandidate = { ...candidate, status: 'interview_scheduled' };
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} candidate={interviewScheduledCandidate} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Schedule Interview')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Approve')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Reject')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render a message when the candidate status is "approved"', () => {
    const approvedCandidate = { ...candidate, status: 'approved' };
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} candidate={approvedCandidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/This candidate has been/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('approved')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render a message when the candidate status is "rejected"', () => {
    const rejectedCandidate = { ...candidate, status: 'rejected' };
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} candidate={rejectedCandidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/This candidate has been/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('rejected')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onScheduleInterview when the "Schedule Interview" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Schedule Interview'));
    expect(onScheduleInterviewMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call onStatusChange with "approved" when the "Approve" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Approve'));
    expect(onStatusChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 'approved');
  });

  it('should call onStatusChange with "rejected" when the "Reject" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Reject'));
    expect(onStatusChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 'rejected');
  });

  it('should disable the buttons when statusUpdateLoading is true', () => {
    render(<ActionsCard {...defaultProps} statusUpdateLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Processing...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Approving...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Rejecting...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Processing...').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Approving...').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Rejecting...').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import ApplicationDetailsCard from '../ApplicationDetailsCard';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ApplicationDetailsCard', () => {
  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    status: 'submitted',
    jobCategory: 'IT',
    specialty: 'Frontend',
    jobTitle: 'job1',
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-01-01T12:00:00Z') },
  };

  it('should render the application details correctly', () => {
    render(<ApplicationDetailsCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Application Details')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Category/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('IT')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Specialty/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Frontend')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Job ID/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('job1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Submitted/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(new Date('2023-01-01T12:00:00Z').toLocaleString())).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Application ID/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('1')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the status badge', () => {
    render(<ApplicationDetailsCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Application Submitted')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/profile-modal/__tests__/DocumentsAndLinksCard.test.tsx
============================================================

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import DocumentsAndLinksCard from '../DocumentsAndLinksCard';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('DocumentsAndLinksCard', () => {
  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    status: 'submitted',
    cvUrl: 'http://example.com/cv.pdf',
    passportUrl: 'http://example.com/passport.pdf',
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() },
  };

  it('should render the "View CV/Resume" and "View ID/Passport" links', () => {
    render(<DocumentsAndLinksCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('View CV/Resume')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('View ID/Passport')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "View Photo" link if photoUrl is provided', () => {
    const candidateWithPhoto = { ...candidate, photoUrl: 'http://example.com/photo.jpg' };
    render(<DocumentsAndLinksCard candidate={candidateWithPhoto} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('View Photo')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should not render the "View Photo" link if photoUrl is not provided', () => {
    render(<DocumentsAndLinksCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('View Photo')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the website links if websites are provided', () => {
    const candidateWithWebsites = { ...candidate, websites: ['http://example.com', 'http://anotherexample.com'] };
    render(<DocumentsAndLinksCard candidate={candidateWithWebsites} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('http://example.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('http://anotherexample.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should not render the website links if websites are not provided', () => {
    render(<DocumentsAndLinksCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('http://example.com')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import InterviewCard from '../InterviewCard';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('InterviewCard', () => {
  const onScheduleInterviewMock = jest.fn();
  const onCancelInterviewMock = jest.fn();

  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    status: 'interview_scheduled',
    interviewDate: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-01-01T12:00:00Z') },
    duration: 45,
    meetLink: 'http://meet.google.com',
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() },
  };

  const defaultProps = {
    candidate,
    onScheduleInterview: onScheduleInterviewMock,
    onCancelInterview: onCancelInterviewMock,
    statusUpdateLoading: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onScheduleInterviewMock.mockClear();
    onCancelInterviewMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should not render if the candidate status is not "interview_scheduled"', () => {
    const { container } = render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} candidate={{ ...candidate, status: 'submitted' }} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
  });

  it('should not render if interviewDate is not provided', () => {
    const { container } = render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} candidate={{ ...candidate, interviewDate: undefined }} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
  });

  it('should render the interview details correctly', () => {
    render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Interview Details')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Date/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Time/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Duration/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('45 minutes')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the "Join Google Meet" link if meetLink is provided', () => {
    render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Join Google Meet')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onScheduleInterview when the "Reschedule" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Reschedule'));
    expect(onScheduleInterviewMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call onCancelInterview when the "Cancel" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Cancel'));
    expect(onCancelInterviewMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 'Cancelled by admin');
  });

  it('should disable the buttons when statusUpdateLoading is true', () => {
    render(<InterviewCard {...defaultProps} statusUpdateLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Reschedule').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Cancel').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';

describe('PersonalInfoCard', () => {
  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    firstName: 'Test',
    lastName: 'User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    gender: 'male',
    countryCode: '+1',
    phoneNumber: '1234567890',
    city: 'Test City',
    country: 'Test Country',
    nationality: 'Test Nationality',
    dateOfBirth: '1990-01-01',
    status: 'submitted',
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() },
  };

  it('should render the personal information correctly', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoCard candidate={candidate} age={33} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Personal Information')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/First Name/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Test')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Last Name/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('User')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Gender/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('male')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Email/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('test@example.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Phone/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('+1 1234567890')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Location/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Test City, Test Country')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Nationality/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Nationality')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Date of Birth/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('1990-01-01 (33 years)')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the second nationality if provided', () => {
    const candidateWithSecondNationality = { ...candidate, doubleNationality: 'Test Second Nationality' };
    render(<PersonalInfoCard candidate={candidateWithSecondNationality} age={33} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/2nd Nationality/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Second Nationality')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should not render the second nationality if not provided', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoCard candidate={candidate} age={33} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText(/2nd Nationality/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the age if dateOfBirth is provided', () => {
    render(<PersonalInfoCard candidate={candidate} age={33} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('1990-01-01 (33 years)')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should not render the age if dateOfBirth is not provided', () => {
    const candidateWithoutDob = { ...candidate, dateOfBirth: undefined };
    render(<PersonalInfoCard candidate={candidateWithoutDob} age={null} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText(/years/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import ProfileHeader from '../ProfileHeader';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ProfileHeader', () => {
  const onEditMock = jest.fn();
  const onCloseMock = jest.fn();

  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    jobTitleText: 'Software Engineer',
    photoUrl: 'http://example.com/photo.jpg',
    status: 'submitted',
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() },
  };

  const defaultProps = {
    candidate,
    onEdit: onEditMock,
    onClose: onCloseMock,
    isUpdating: false,
    statusUpdateLoading: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onEditMock.mockClear();
    onCloseMock.mockClear();
  });

  it("should render the candidate's full name and job title", () => {
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test User')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Software Engineer')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("should render the candidate's photo if photoUrl is provided", () => {
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} />);
    const image = screen.getByRole('img');
    expect(image).toHaveAttribute('src', 'http://example.com/photo.jpg');
  });

  it('should render a placeholder icon if photoUrl is not provided', () => {
    const candidateWithoutPhoto = { ...candidate, photoUrl: undefined };
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} candidate={candidateWithoutPhoto} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('img')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('icon')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onEdit when the "Edit" button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Edit'));
    expect(onEditMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call onClose when the close button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '' }));
    expect(onCloseMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should disable the "Edit" button when isUpdating is true', () => {
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} isUpdating={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Edit').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('should disable the "Edit" button when statusUpdateLoading is true', () => {
    render(<ProfileHeader {...defaultProps} statusUpdateLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Edit').closest('button')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import ScoringCard from '../ScoringCard';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ScoringCard', () => {
  const candidate = {
    id: '1',
    fullName: 'Test User',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    status: 'submitted',
    score: 85,
    aiRecommendation: 'highly_recommended',
    scoreAnalysis: 'This is a great candidate.',
    keyStrengths: ['React', 'TypeScript'],
    areasForImprovement: ['Testing'],
    scoringVersion: '1.0',
    lastScoredAt: { toDate: () => new Date('2023-01-01') },
    submittedAt: { toDate: () => new Date() },
  };

  it('should not render if the candidate score is null or undefined', () => {
    const { container } = render(<ScoringCard candidate={{ ...candidate, score: null }} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
  });

  it('should render the AI score and recommendation', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('85')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Highly Recommended')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the analysis if provided', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('This is a great candidate.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the key strengths if provided', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('React')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('TypeScript')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the areas for improvement if provided', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Testing')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render the metadata if provided', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={candidate} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Analyzed:/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('1.0')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should display the correct recommendation text and color for "recommended"', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={{ ...candidate, aiRecommendation: 'recommended' }} />);
    const recommendation = screen.getByText('Recommended');
    expect(recommendation).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(recommendation.parentElement).toHaveClass('text-blue-600');
  });

  it('should display the correct recommendation text and color for "conditional"', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={{ ...candidate, aiRecommendation: 'conditional' }} />);
    const recommendation = screen.getByText('Conditional');
    expect(recommendation).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(recommendation.parentElement).toHaveClass('text-yellow-600');
  });

  it('should display the correct recommendation text and color for "not_recommended"', () => {
    render(<ScoringCard candidate={{ ...candidate, aiRecommendation: 'not_recommended' }} />);
    const recommendation = screen.getByText('Not Recommended');
    expect(recommendation).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(recommendation.parentElement).toHaveClass('text-red-600');
  });
});

FILE: app/components/profile-modal/ActionsCard.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiEdit, FiCheckCircle, FiXCircle, FiCalendar } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard } from './InfoCard';

interface ActionsCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string) => void;
    onScheduleInterview: () => void;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
}

const ActionsCard: React.FC<ActionsCardProps> = ({
    candidate,
    onStatusChange,
    onScheduleInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
}) => {
    const handleReject = () => onStatusChange(candidate.id, 'rejected');
    const handleApprove = () => onStatusChange(candidate.id, 'approved');

    const renderActionButtons = () => {
        if (candidate.status === 'approved' || candidate.status === 'rejected') {
            return (
                <div className="text-center py-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 italic">
                    This candidate has been <strong>{candidate.status}</strong>.
                </div>
            );
        }

        return (
            <div className="space-y-3">
                {candidate.status === 'submitted' && (
                     <button onClick={onScheduleInterview} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                        <FiCalendar className="mr-2" /> {statusUpdateLoading ? 'Processing...' : 'Schedule Interview'}
                    </button>
                )}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <button onClick={handleApprove} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                        <FiCheckCircle className="mr-2" /> {statusUpdateLoading ? 'Approving...' : 'Approve'}
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={handleReject} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                        <FiXCircle className="mr-2" /> {statusUpdateLoading ? 'Rejecting...' : 'Reject'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        );
    };

    return (
        <InfoCard title="Actions" icon={<FiEdit size={20} />}>
            {renderActionButtons()}
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default ActionsCard;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiFileText, FiClipboard, FiImage, FiLink } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard } from './InfoCard';

interface DocumentsAndLinksCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
}

const DocumentsAndLinksCard: React.FC<DocumentsAndLinksCardProps> = ({ candidate }) => {
    return (
        <InfoCard title="Documents & Links" icon={<FiFileText size={20} />}>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <a href={candidate.cvUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors">
                    <FiFileText className="mr-2" /> View CV/Resume
                </a>
                <a href={candidate.passportUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors">
                    <FiClipboard className="mr-2" /> View ID/Passport
                </a>
                {candidate.photoUrl && (
                    <a href={candidate.photoUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors">
                        <FiImage className="mr-2" /> View Photo
                    </a>
                )}
            </div>
            {candidate.websites && candidate.websites.length > 0 && (
                <div className="pt-3 space-y-2">
                    {candidate.websites.map((site, index) => (
                        <a key={index} href={site.startsWith('http') ? site : `https://${site}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline break-all">
                            <FiLink size={14} className="mr-2" /> {site}
                        </a>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default DocumentsAndLinksCard;

FILE: app/components/profile-modal/InfoCard.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';

export const InfoCard: React.FC<{ title: string; icon: React.ReactNode; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ title, icon, children }) => (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-sm">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex items-center">
            <div className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mr-3">{icon}</div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{title}</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">{children}</div>
    </div>
);

export const InfoRow: React.FC<{ label: string; value?: string | null; icon?: React.ReactNode }> = ({ label, value, icon }) => {
    if (!value && value !== null) return null;
    return (
        <div className="flex items-start">
            {icon && <div className="text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-0.5 mr-2">{icon}</div>}
            <span className="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200 w-32 flex-shrink-0">{label}:</span>
            <span className="flex-1 break-words">{value ?? 'N/A'}</span>
        </div>
    );
};

FILE: app/components/profile-modal/InterviewCard.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { FiCalendar, FiClock, FiExternalLink, FiEdit, FiTrash2 } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard, InfoRow } from './InfoCard';

interface InterviewCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onScheduleInterview: () => void;
    onCancelInterview: (candidateId: string, reason?: string) => void;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
}

const InterviewCard: React.FC<InterviewCardProps> = ({
    candidate,
    onScheduleInterview,
    onCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
}) => {
    if (candidate?.status !== 'interview_scheduled' || !candidate?.interviewDate) {
        return null;
    }

    const interviewDate = candidate.interviewDate.toDate();
    const duration = candidate.duration || 30;
    const endDate = new Date(interviewDate.getTime() + duration * 60 * 1000);

    return (
        <InfoCard title="Interview Details" icon={<FiCalendar size={20} />}>
            <InfoRow label="Date" value={format(interviewDate, 'PPP')} icon={<FiClock size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Time" value={`${format(interviewDate, 'p')} - ${format(endDate, 'p')}`} icon={<FiClock size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Duration" value={`${duration} minutes`} icon={<FiClock size={14} />} />
            {candidate.meetLink && (
                <div className="pt-2">
                    <a href={candidate.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors w-full justify-center">
                        <FiExternalLink className="mr-2" /> Join Google Meet
                    </a>
                </div>
            )}
            <div className="pt-3 flex space-x-2">
                <button onClick={onScheduleInterview} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-200 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/80 disabled:opacity-50">
                    <FiEdit className="mr-2" /> Reschedule
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => onCancelInterview(candidate.id, 'Cancelled by admin')} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-700 dark:text-red-300 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/50 rounded-md hover:bg-red-200 dark:hover:bg-red-900/80 disabled:opacity-50">
                    <FiTrash2 className="mr-2" /> Cancel
                </button>
            </div>
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default InterviewCard;

============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiUser, FiUsers, FiMail, FiPhone, FiMapPin, FiGlobe, FiCalendar } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard, InfoRow } from './InfoCard';

interface PersonalInfoCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    age: number | null;
}

const PersonalInfoCard: React.FC<PersonalInfoCardProps> = ({ candidate, age }) => {
    return (
        <InfoCard title="Personal Information" icon={<FiUser size={20} />}>
            <InfoRow label="First Name" value={candidate.firstName} />
            <InfoRow label="Last Name" value={candidate.lastName} />
            <InfoRow label="Gender" value={candidate.gender} icon={<FiUsers size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Email" value={candidate.email} icon={<FiMail size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Phone" value={candidate.phoneNumber ? `${candidate.countryCode || ''} ${candidate.phoneNumber}` : null} icon={<FiPhone size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Location" value={candidate.city && candidate.country ? `${candidate.city}, ${candidate.country}` : null} icon={<FiMapPin size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Nationality" value={candidate.nationality} icon={<FiGlobe size={14} />} />
            {candidate.doubleNationality && <InfoRow label="2nd Nationality" value={candidate.doubleNationality} icon={<FiGlobe size={14} />} />}
            <InfoRow label="Date of Birth" value={candidate.dateOfBirth ? `${candidate.dateOfBirth} (${age} years)` : null} icon={<FiCalendar size={14} />} />
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default PersonalInfoCard;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiX, FiUser, FiEdit } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface ProfileHeaderProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onClose: () => void;
    onEdit: () => void;
    isUpdating: boolean;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
}

const ProfileHeader: React.FC<ProfileHeaderProps> = ({ candidate, onClose, onEdit, isUpdating, statusUpdateLoading }) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center flex-shrink-0">
            <div className="flex items-center">
                {candidate.photoUrl ? (
                    <img src={candidate.photoUrl} alt={candidate.fullName} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover mr-4 border-2 border-white dark:border-gray-700 shadow" />
                ) : (
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-4" data-testid="icon"><FiUser size={24} className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400" /></div>
                )}
                <div>
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{candidate.fullName}</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{candidate.jobTitleText}</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <button
                    onClick={onEdit}
                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-300 bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-200 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/80 transition-colors"
                    disabled={statusUpdateLoading || isUpdating}
                >
                    <FiEdit className="mr-2" size={16} />
                    Edit
                </button>
                <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors rounded-full p-2">
                    <FiX size={24} />
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ProfileHeader;

"use client";

import React from 'react';

import DocumentsAndLinksCard from './DocumentsAndLinksCard';

import InterviewCard from './InterviewCard';
import ActionsCard from './ActionsCard';

interface ProfileModalContentProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    age: number | null;
    onStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string, interviewTimestampISO?: string) => Promise<void>;
    onCancelInterview: (candidateId: string, reason?: string) => Promise<void>;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
    onScheduleInterview: () => void;
}

const ProfileModalContent: React.FC<ProfileModalContentProps> = ({
    candidate,
    age,
    onStatusChange,
    onCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    onScheduleInterview,
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto flex-grow">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

                    <ApplicationDetailsCard candidate={candidate} />
                    <DocumentsAndLinksCard candidate={candidate} />
                    <SystemDetailsCard candidate={candidate} />

                <div className="space-y-6">
                    <ScoringCard candidate={candidate} />
                    <InterviewCard
                        candidate={candidate}
                        onScheduleInterview={onScheduleInterview}
                        onCancelInterview={onCancelInterview}
                        statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                    />
                    <ActionsCard
                        candidate={candidate}
                        onStatusChange={onStatusChange}
                        onScheduleInterview={onScheduleInterview}
                        statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

FILE: app/components/profile-modal/ScoringCard.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiAward, FiTrendingUp, FiAlertTriangle, FiCheckCircle, FiXCircle } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard } from './InfoCard';

interface ScoringCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
}

const ScoringCard: React.FC<ScoringCardProps> = ({ candidate }) => {
    if (candidate.score === null || candidate.score === undefined) {
        return null;
    }

    // Get recommendation color and icon
    const getRecommendationDisplay = (recommendation?: string) => {
        switch (recommendation) {
            case 'highly_recommended':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-green-600 bg-green-100', 
                    icon: <FiCheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Highly Recommended'
                };
            case 'recommended':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-blue-600 bg-blue-100', 
                    icon: <FiTrendingUp className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Recommended'
                };
            case 'conditional':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-yellow-600 bg-yellow-100', 
                    icon: <FiAlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Conditional'
                };
            case 'not_recommended':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-red-600 bg-red-100', 
                    icon: <FiXCircle className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Not Recommended'
                };
            default:
                return { 
                    color: 'text-gray-600 bg-gray-100', 
                    icon: <FiAward className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Evaluated'
                };
        }
    };

    const recommendationDisplay = getRecommendationDisplay(candidate.aiRecommendation);

    // Get score color based on value
    const getScoreColor = (score: number) => {
        if (score >= 80) return 'text-green-600';
        if (score >= 60) return 'text-blue-600';
        if (score >= 40) return 'text-yellow-600';
        return 'text-red-600';
    };

    const scoreColor = getScoreColor(candidate.score);

    return (
        <InfoCard title="🤖 AI Analysis (Gemini 2.5 Pro)" icon={<FiAward size={20} />}>
            <div className="space-y-4">
                {/* Score and Recommendation Header */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                        <div className="relative w-16 h-16">
                            <svg className="w-full h-full transform -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 36 36">
                                <path 
                                    className="text-gray-200" 
                                    strokeWidth="3" 
                                    fill="none" 
                                    d="M18 2.0845 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 31.831 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 -31.831" 
                                />
                                <path 
                                    className={scoreColor}
                                    strokeWidth="3" 
                                    fill="none" 
                                    strokeDasharray={`${candidate.score}, 100`} 
                                    d="M18 2.0845 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 31.831 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 -31.831" 
                                />
                            </svg>
                            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                                <span className={`text-xl font-bold ${scoreColor}`}>{candidate.score}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600">AI Score</p>
                            {candidate.aiRecommendation && (
                                <div className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${recommendationDisplay.color} dark:bg-opacity-20`}>
                                    {recommendationDisplay.icon}
                                    <span>{recommendationDisplay.text}</span>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Analysis */}
                {candidate.scoreAnalysis && (
                    <div>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-2">📋 Analysis</h4>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">{candidate.scoreAnalysis}</p>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Key Strengths */}
                {candidate.keyStrengths && candidate.keyStrengths.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-2 flex items-center">
                            <span className="text-green-600 mr-1">✅</span> Key Strengths
                        </h4>
                        <ul className="space-y-1">
                            {candidate.keyStrengths.map((strength, index) => (
                                <li key={index} className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 flex items-start">
                                    <span className="text-green-500 mr-2 mt-1">•</span>
                                    <span>{strength}</span>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Areas for Improvement */}
                {candidate.areasForImprovement && candidate.areasForImprovement.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-2 flex items-center">
                            <span className="text-yellow-600 mr-1">⚠️</span> Development Areas
                        </h4>
                        <ul className="space-y-1">
                            {candidate.areasForImprovement.map((area, index) => (
                                <li key={index} className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 flex items-start">
                                    <span className="text-yellow-500 mr-2 mt-1">•</span>
                                    <span>{area}</span>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Metadata */}
                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                        <span>
                            {candidate.lastScoredAt && 
                                `Analyzed: ${candidate.lastScoredAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString()}`
                            }
                        </span>
                        {candidate.scoringVersion && (
                            <span className="bg-purple-100 dark:bg-purple-900/50 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                                {candidate.scoringVersion}
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default ScoringCard;

FILE: app/components/ui/__tests__/ConfirmationModal.test.tsx
============================================================

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import ConfirmationModal from '../ConfirmationModal';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('ConfirmationModal', () => {
  const onConfirmMock = jest.fn();
  const onCancelMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    isOpen: true,
    title: 'Test Title',
    message: 'Test Message',
    onConfirm: onConfirmMock,
    onCancel: onCancelMock,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onConfirmMock.mockClear();
    onCancelMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should not render when isOpen is false', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} isOpen={false} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Test Title')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render correctly with title and message when isOpen is true', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Title')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Message')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onConfirm when the confirm button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Confirm'));
    expect(onConfirmMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call onCancel when the cancel button is clicked', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Cancel'));
    expect(onCancelMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should display correct styles for danger type', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} type="danger" />);
    const confirmButton = screen.getByText('Confirm');
    expect(confirmButton).toHaveClass('bg-red-600');
  });

  it('should display correct styles for warning type', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} type="warning" />);
    const confirmButton = screen.getByText('Confirm');
    expect(confirmButton).toHaveClass('bg-yellow-600');
  });

  it('should display correct styles for info type', () => {
    render(<ConfirmationModal {...defaultProps} type="info" />);
    const confirmButton = screen.getByText('Confirm');
    expect(confirmButton).toHaveClass('bg-blue-600');
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import FileInput from '../FileInput';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('FileInput', () => {
  const onChangeMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    name: 'test-file',
    label: 'Test File',
    accept: 'image/*',
    onChange: onChangeMock,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onChangeMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render correctly with the provided label', () => {
    render(<FileInput {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Test File')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onChange when a file is selected', () => {
    render(<FileInput {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Test File');
    const file = new File(['(⌐□_□)'], 'chucknorris.png', { type: 'image/png' });
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { files: [file] } });
    expect(onChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should be disabled when the disabled prop is true', () => {
    render(<FileInput {...defaultProps} disabled={true} />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Test File');
    expect(input).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('should show a * when the required prop is true', () => {
    render(<FileInput {...defaultProps} required={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('*')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/ui/__tests__/SearchableDropdown.test.tsx
============================================================

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import SearchableDropdown from '../SearchableDropdown';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('SearchableDropdown', () => {
  const onChangeMock = jest.fn();
  const options = ['Apple', 'Banana', 'Cherry'];

  const defaultProps = {
    options,
    value: '',
    onChange: onChangeMock,
    placeholder: 'Select a fruit',
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onChangeMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render correctly with the provided placeholder', () => {
    render(<SearchableDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Select a fruit')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should show the options when the input is focused', () => {
    render(<SearchableDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText('Select a fruit');
    fireEvent.focus(input);
    expect(screen.getByText('Apple')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Banana')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Cherry')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should filter the options based on the search input', () => {
    render(<SearchableDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText('Select a fruit');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'a' } });
    expect(screen.getByText('Apple')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Banana')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Cherry')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onChange with the selected option when an option is clicked', () => {
    render(<SearchableDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText('Select a fruit');
    fireEvent.focus(input);
    const appleOption = screen.getByText('Apple');
    fireEvent.click(appleOption);
    expect(onChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Apple');
  });

  it('should hide the options when an option is clicked', () => {
    render(<SearchableDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText('Select a fruit');
    fireEvent.focus(input);
    const appleOption = screen.getByText('Apple');
    fireEvent.click(appleOption);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Banana')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should hide the options when clicking outside the component', () => {
    render(
      <div>
        <SearchableDropdown {...defaultProps} />
        <button>Outside</button>
      </div>
    );
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText('Select a fruit');
    fireEvent.focus(input);
    expect(screen.getByText('Apple')).toBeInTheDocument();
    fireEvent.mouseDown(screen.getByText('Outside'));
    expect(screen.queryByText('Apple')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent, act } from '@testing-library/react';
import SuccessToast from '../SuccessToast';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

jest.useFakeTimers();

describe('SuccessToast', () => {
  const onCloseMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    message: 'Test Message',
    isVisible: true,
    onClose: onCloseMock,
    duration: 3000,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onCloseMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should not render when isVisible is false', () => {
    render(<SuccessToast {...defaultProps} isVisible={false} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Test Message')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render correctly with the provided message when isVisible is true', () => {
    render(<SuccessToast {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Message')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Application Submitted!')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onClose after the duration', () => {
    render(<SuccessToast {...defaultProps} />);
    act(() => {
      jest.advanceTimersByTime(3300);
    });
    expect(onCloseMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call onClose when the close button is clicked', () => {
    render(<SuccessToast {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(/Close/));
    act(() => {
        jest.advanceTimersByTime(300); // for fade out animation
    });
    expect(onCloseMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
