import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import Tabs from '../Tabs';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('Tabs', () => {
  const onTabChangeMock = jest.fn();
  const tabs = [
    { label: 'Tab 1', value: 'tab1' },
    { label: 'Tab 2', value: 'tab2' },
  ];

  const defaultProps = {
    activeTab: 'tab1',
    onTabChange: onTabChangeMock,
    tabs,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onTabChangeMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should render the tabs correctly', () => {
    render(<Tabs {...defaultProps}>Content</Tabs>);
    expect(screen.getByText('Tab 1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Tab 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onTabChange when a tab is clicked', () => {
    render(<Tabs {...defaultProps}>Content</Tabs>);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Tab 2'));
    expect(onTabChangeMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('tab2');
  });

  it('should display the correct active tab', () => {
    render(<Tabs {...defaultProps}>Content</Tabs>);
    const tab1 = screen.getByText('Tab 1');
    expect(tab1).toHaveClass('bg-white');
  });

  it('should render the children content', () => {
    render(<Tabs {...defaultProps}>Content</Tabs>);
    expect(screen.getByText('Content')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: app/components/ui/__tests__/Toast.test.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent, act } from '@testing-library/react';
import Toast from '../Toast';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

jest.useFakeTimers();

describe('Toast', () => {
  const onCloseMock = jest.fn();

  const defaultProps = {
    message: 'Test Message',
    type: 'success' as const,
    isVisible: true,
    onClose: onCloseMock,
    duration: 3000,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    onCloseMock.mockClear();
  });

  it('should not render when isVisible is false', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} isVisible={false} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Test Message')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should render correctly with the provided message and type', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Message')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('✓')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('should call onClose after the duration', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} />);
    act(() => {
      jest.advanceTimersByTime(3000);
    });
    expect(onCloseMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should call onClose when the close button is clicked', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('✕'));
    expect(onCloseMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should render correct styles for success type', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} type="success" />);
    const toast = screen.getByTestId('toast');
    expect(toast).toHaveClass('bg-green-500');
  });

  it('should render correct styles for error type', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} type="error" />);
    const toast = screen.getByTestId('toast');
    expect(toast).toHaveClass('bg-red-500');
  });

  it('should render correct styles for warning type', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} type="warning" />);
    const toast = screen.getByTestId('toast');
    expect(toast).toHaveClass('bg-yellow-500');
  });

  it('should render correct styles for info type', () => {
    render(<Toast {...defaultProps} type="info" />);
    const toast = screen.getByTestId('toast');
    expect(toast).toHaveClass('bg-blue-500');
  });
});

// src/app/components/ConfirmationModal.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { ConfirmationModalProps } from '@/types';

const ConfirmationModal: React.FC<ConfirmationModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  title,
  message,
  confirmText = 'Confirm',
  cancelText = 'Cancel',
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  type = 'danger'
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const getButtonStyles = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'danger':
        return 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500';
      case 'warning':
        return 'bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-700 focus:ring-yellow-500';
      case 'info':
        return 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 focus:ring-blue-500';
      default:
        return 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500';
    }
  };

  const getIconColor = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'danger':
        return 'text-red-600';
      case 'warning':
        return 'text-yellow-600';
      case 'info':
        return 'text-blue-600';
      default:
        return 'text-red-600';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center z-50">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-md w-full mx-4">
        <div className="flex items-center mb-4">
          <div className={`flex-shrink-0 w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${type === 'danger' ? 'bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/50' : type === 'warning' ? 'bg-yellow-100 dark:bg-yellow-900/50' : 'bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/50'}`}>
            <span className={`text-xl ${getIconColor()}`}>
              {type === 'danger' ? '⚠' : type === 'warning' ? '⚠' : 'ℹ'}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h3 className="ml-3 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{title}</h3>
        </div>
        
        <div className="mb-6">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{message}</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex justify-end space-x-3">
          <button
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
          >
            {cancelText}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onConfirm}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 ${getButtonStyles()}`}
          >
            {confirmText}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ConfirmationModal;

FILE: app/components/ui/SearchableDropdown.tsx
============================================================
// src/app/components/ui/SearchableDropdown.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { SearchableDropdownProps } from '@/types';

const SearchableDropdown: React.FC<SearchableDropdownProps> = ({
  options,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  disabled = false,
  id,
}) => {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(value);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSearch(value);
  }, [value]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  const filteredOptions = options.filter(option =>
    option.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={ref}>
      <input
        id={id}
        type="text"
        value={search}
        onChange={(e) => {
          setSearch(e.target.value);
          onChange('');
          setShowList(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setShowList(true)}
        className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
        placeholder={placeholder}
        disabled={disabled}
        autoComplete="off"
      />
      {showList && filteredOptions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute z-10 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredOptions.map((option, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onChange(option);
                setSearch(option);
                setShowList(false);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 text-left hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
            >
              {option}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SearchableDropdown;

// src/app/components/ui/StatusBadge.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { StatusBadgeProps } from '@/types';

const StatusBadge: React.FC<StatusBadgeProps> = ({ status }) => {
  const getStatusStyle = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'submitted':
        return 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-900/50 dark:text-blue-300 dark:border-blue-500/30';
      case 'interview_scheduled':
        return 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border-yellow-200 dark:bg-yellow-900/50 dark:text-yellow-300 dark:border-yellow-500/30';
      case 'approved':
        return 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200 dark:bg-green-900/50 dark:text-green-300 dark:border-green-500/30';
      case 'rejected':
        return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-200 dark:bg-red-900/50 dark:text-red-300 dark:border-red-500/30';
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800 border-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600';
    }
  };

  const getStatusText = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'submitted':
        return 'Application Submitted';
      case 'interview_scheduled':
        return 'Interview Scheduled';
      case 'approved':
        return 'Approved';
      case 'rejected':
        return 'Rejected';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm font-medium border ${getStatusStyle(status)}`}>
      {getStatusText(status)}
    </span>
  );
};

export default StatusBadge;

FILE: app/components/ui/SuccessToast.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { SuccessToastProps } from '@/types';

const SuccessToast: React.FC<SuccessToastProps & { duration?: number }> = ({ 
  message, 
  isVisible, 
  onClose, 
  duration = 5000 
}) => {
  const [isAnimating, setIsAnimating] = useState(false);
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(Math.floor(duration / 1000));
  const timerRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isVisible) {
      setIsAnimating(true);
      setCountdown(Math.floor(duration / 1000));
      
      // Countdown timer
      const countdownInterval = setInterval(() => {
        setCountdown(prev => {
          if (prev <= 1) {
            clearInterval(countdownInterval);
            return 0;
          }
          return prev - 1;
        });
      }, 1000);
      
      // Auto close timer
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setIsAnimating(false);
        setTimeout(onClose, 300); // Wait for fade out animation
      }, duration);
      
      return () => {
        if(timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
        clearInterval(countdownInterval);
      };
    }
  }, [isVisible, onClose, duration]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const handleClose = () => {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    }
    setIsAnimating(false);
    setTimeout(onClose, 300);
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 pointer-events-none">
      {/* Backdrop with subtle blur */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/10 backdrop-blur-sm"></div>
      
      {/* Toast Container */}
      <div className={`
        relative bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 
        max-w-md w-full mx-4 transform transition-all duration-500 ease-out
        ${isAnimating 
          ? 'scale-100 opacity-100 translate-y-0' 
          : 'scale-95 opacity-0 translate-y-4'
        }
      `}>
        {/* Success Header with gradient */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#003A70] to-[#004080] text-white p-6 rounded-t-2xl">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            {/* Animated Success Icon */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white/20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                <svg 
                  className="w-8 h-8 text-white animate-bounce" 
                  fill="none" 
                  stroke="currentColor" 
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                >
                  <path 
                    strokeLinecap="round" 
                    strokeLinejoin="round" 
                    strokeWidth={2.5} 
                    d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" 
                  />
                </svg>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Application Submitted!</h3>
              <p className="text-white/90 text-sm mt-1">Redirecting to your dashboard...</p>
            </div>
            {/* Countdown circle */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-2 border-white/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm">{countdown}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Message Content */}
        <div className="p-6">
          <p className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-base leading-relaxed mb-4">
            {message}
          </p>
          
          {/* Success indicators */}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-green-500 rounded-full mr-3 animate-pulse"></div>
              Application received and being processed
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full mr-3 animate-pulse"></div>
              You'll be redirected to your dashboard shortly
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-purple-500 rounded-full mr-3 animate-pulse"></div>
              Our team will review your application
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Bottom section with action */}
        <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-700/50 px-6 py-4 rounded-b-2xl border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Application ID: #APP{Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}
            </p>
            <button
              onClick={handleClose}
              className="text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 hover:text-[#002B54] dark:hover:text-blue-300 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors"
            >
              Close ({countdown}s)
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Decorative elements */}
        <div className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 w-6 h-6 bg-yellow-400 rounded-full animate-ping opacity-75"></div>
        <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-yellow-500 rounded-full"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SuccessToast;

// src/app/components/Tabs.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { TabProps, TabsProps } from '@/types';

const Tab: React.FC<TabProps> = ({ label, active, onClick }) => {
  return (
    <button
      className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium rounded-t-lg focus:outline-none ${
        active
          ? 'bg-white text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600'
          : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-800'
      }`}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      {label}
    </button>
  );
};

const Tabs: React.FC<TabsProps> = ({ activeTab, onTabChange, tabs, children }) => {
  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <div className="border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
        <nav className="-mb-px flex space-x-8" aria-label="Tabs">
          {tabs.map((tab) => (
            <Tab
              key={tab.value}
              label={tab.label}
              active={activeTab === tab.value}
              onClick={() => onTabChange(tab.value)}
            />
          ))}
        </nav>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Tabs;

"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";
import { Sun, Moon } from "lucide-react";

export function ThemeSwitcher() {
  const { setTheme, theme } = useTheme();

  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => setTheme(theme === "light" ? "dark" : "light")}
      className="relative p-2 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors"
    >
      <Sun className="h-[1.2rem] w-[1.2rem] rotate-0 scale-100 transition-all dark:-rotate-90 dark:scale-0" />
      <Moon className="absolute top-2 left-2 h-[1.2rem] w-[1.2rem] rotate-90 scale-0 transition-all dark:rotate-0 dark:scale-100" />
      <span className="sr-only">Toggle theme</span>
    </button>
  );
}

// src/app/components/Toast.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ToastProps } from '@/types';

const Toast: React.FC<ToastProps> = ({ 
  message, 
  type, 
  isVisible, 
  onClose, 
  duration = 5000 
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isVisible && duration > 0) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        onClose();
      }, duration);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [isVisible, duration, onClose]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const getToastStyles = () => {
    const baseStyles = "fixed top-4 right-4 p-4 rounded-lg shadow-lg z-50 max-w-sm w-full transform transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out";
    
    switch (type) {
      case 'success':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-green-500 text-white`;
      case 'error':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-red-500 text-white`;
      case 'warning':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-yellow-500 text-white`;
      case 'info':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-blue-500 text-white`;
      default:
        return `${baseStyles} bg-gray-500 text-white`;
    }
  };

  const getIcon = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'success':
        return '✓';
      case 'error':
        return '✕';
      case 'warning':
        return '⚠';
      case 'info':
        return 'ℹ';
      default:
        return '';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={getToastStyles()} data-testid="toast">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center">
          <span className="text-lg mr-2">{getIcon()}</span>
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">{message}</span>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="ml-4 text-white hover:text-gray-200 focus:outline-none"
        >
          ✕
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Toast;

FILE: app/components/CandidateFilters.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { Candidate, CandidateFiltersProps } from '@/types';
import SearchableFilterDropdown from './common/SearchableFilterDropdown';
import QuickFilterButtons from '@/app/components/common/QuickFilterButtons';

const CandidateFilters: React.FC<CandidateFiltersProps> = ({
  candidates,
  filterStatus,
  filterName,
  filterCity,
  filterCountry,
  filterNationality,
  filterJobTitle,
  filterCategory,
  filterSpecialty,
  filterAgeMin,
  filterAgeMax,
  onFilterStatusChange,
  onFilterNameChange,
  onFilterCityChange,
  onFilterCountryChange,
  onFilterNationalityChange,
  onFilterJobTitleChange,
  onFilterCategoryChange,
  onFilterSpecialtyChange,
  onFilterAgeMinChange,
  onFilterAgeMaxChange,
  onClearAllFilters
}) => {

  // Search states for interactive dropdowns
  const [citySearch, setCitySearch] = useState(filterCity);
  const [countrySearch, setCountrySearch] = useState(filterCountry);
  const [jobTitleSearch, setJobTitleSearch] = useState(filterJobTitle);
  const [categorySearch, setCategorySearch] = useState(filterCategory);
  const [nationalitySearch, setNationalitySearch] = useState(filterNationality);
  const [specialtySearch, setSpecialtySearch] = useState(filterSpecialty);

  // Update search states when filters change externally
  useEffect(() => {
    setCitySearch(filterCity);
  }, [filterCity]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCountrySearch(filterCountry);
  }, [filterCountry]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setJobTitleSearch(filterJobTitle);
  }, [filterJobTitle]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCategorySearch(filterCategory);
  }, [filterCategory]);
 
  useEffect(() => {
    setNationalitySearch(filterNationality);
  }, [filterNationality]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSpecialtySearch(filterSpecialty);
  }, [filterSpecialty]);

  // Extract unique values from candidates
  const uniqueCities = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.city).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueCountries = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.country).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueJobTitles = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.jobTitleText).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueCategories = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.jobCategory).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueNationalities = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.nationality).filter((n): n is string => !!n))).sort();
  const uniqueSpecialties = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.specialty).filter((s): s is string => !!s))).sort();

  const handleClearAllFilters = () => {
    setCitySearch('');
    setCountrySearch('');
    setJobTitleSearch('');
    setCategorySearch('');
    setNationalitySearch('');
    setSpecialtySearch('');
    onClearAllFilters();
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 mb-6">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-4">Filters:</h3>
      
      {/* Main Filter Inputs */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4 mb-6">
        {/* Name Filter */}
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Filter by Name</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Filter by name..."
            value={filterName}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterNameChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          />
        </div>
        
        {/* City Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by City"
          placeholder="Search and select city..."
          filterValue={filterCity}
          onFilterChange={onFilterCityChange}
          searchValue={citySearch}
          onSearchChange={setCitySearch}
          options={uniqueCities}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="city"
        />
        
        {/* Country Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Country"
          placeholder="Search and select country..."
          filterValue={filterCountry}
          onFilterChange={onFilterCountryChange}
          searchValue={countrySearch}
          onSearchChange={setCountrySearch}
          options={uniqueCountries}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="country"
        />
        
        {/* Nationality Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Nationality"
          placeholder="Search and select nationality..."
          filterValue={filterNationality}
          onFilterChange={onFilterNationalityChange}
          searchValue={nationalitySearch}
          onSearchChange={setNationalitySearch}
          options={uniqueNationalities}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="nationality"
        />
        
        {/* Category Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Category"
          placeholder="Search and select category..."
          filterValue={filterCategory}
          onFilterChange={onFilterCategoryChange}
          searchValue={categorySearch}
          onSearchChange={setCategorySearch}
          options={uniqueCategories}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="jobCategory"
        />

        {/* Specialty Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Specialty"
          placeholder="Search and select specialty..."
          filterValue={filterSpecialty}
          onFilterChange={onFilterSpecialtyChange}
          searchValue={specialtySearch}
          onSearchChange={setSpecialtySearch}
          options={uniqueSpecialties}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="specialty"
        />

        {/* Job Title Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Job Title"
          placeholder="Search and select job title..."
          filterValue={filterJobTitle}
          onFilterChange={onFilterJobTitleChange}
          searchValue={jobTitleSearch}
          onSearchChange={setJobTitleSearch}
          options={uniqueJobTitles}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="jobTitleText"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Age and Status Filters */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-6">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Min Age</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            placeholder="Min age..."
            value={filterAgeMin}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterAgeMinChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          />
        </div>
        
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Max Age</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            placeholder="Max age..."
            value={filterAgeMax}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterAgeMaxChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          />
        </div>
        
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="status-filter" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Filter by Status</label>
          <select
            id="status-filter"
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterStatusChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          >
            <option value="all">All Statuses</option>
            <option value="submitted">Submitted</option>
            <option value="interview_scheduled">Interview Scheduled</option>
            <option value="rejected">Rejected</option>
            <option value="approved">Approved</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Clear All Filters Button */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <button
          onClick={handleClearAllFilters}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-600 dark:bg-gray-700 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-700 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-gray-500 transition-colors"
        >
          Clear All Filters
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Quick Filters */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <QuickFilterButtons
          label="Status"
          options={['all', 'submitted', 'interview_scheduled', 'rejected', 'approved']}
          filterValue={filterStatus}
          onFilterChange={onFilterStatusChange}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="status"
          colorClass="gray"
        />

        {candidates.length > 0 && uniqueSpecialties.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Popular Specialties"
            options={uniqueSpecialties}
            filterValue={filterSpecialty}
            onFilterChange={onFilterSpecialtyChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="specialty"
            colorClass="emerald"
            sliceLimit={8}
          />
        )}
        
        {candidates.length > 0 && uniqueNationalities.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Nationalities"
            options={uniqueNationalities}
            filterValue={filterNationality}
            onFilterChange={onFilterNationalityChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="nationality"
            colorClass="pink"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Popular Cities"
            options={uniqueCities}
            filterValue={filterCity}
            onFilterChange={onFilterCityChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="city"
            colorClass="indigo"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Popular Countries"
            options={uniqueCountries}
            filterValue={filterCountry}
            onFilterChange={onFilterCountryChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="country"
            colorClass="purple"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Job Categories"
            options={uniqueCategories}
            filterValue={filterCategory}
            onFilterChange={onFilterCategoryChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="jobCategory"
            colorClass="orange"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Job Positions"
            options={uniqueJobTitles}
            filterValue={filterJobTitle}
            onFilterChange={onFilterJobTitleChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="jobTitleText"
            colorClass="teal"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default CandidateFilters;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { useProfileModal } from '@/hooks/useProfileModal';
import type { CandidateProfileModalProps } from '@/types';
import EditCandidateModal from './EditCandidateModal';
import ProfileHeader from './profile-modal/ProfileHeader';
import ProfileModalContent from './profile-modal/ProfileModalContent';

const CandidateProfileModal: React.FC<CandidateProfileModalProps> = ({
    isOpen,
    onClose,
    candidate,
    onStatusChange,
    onCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    onScheduleInterview,
    onEditCandidate,
}) => {
    const {
        isEditModalOpen,
        setIsEditModalOpen,
        isUpdating,
        handleEditCandidate,
        calculateAge,
    } = useProfileModal(candidate, onEditCandidate);

    if (!isOpen || !candidate) return null;

    const age = candidate.dateOfBirth ? calculateAge(candidate.dateOfBirth) : null;

    return (
        <>
            <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-60 flex justify-center items-center z-50 p-4">
                <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-5xl w-full max-h-[95vh] flex flex-col">
                    <ProfileHeader
                        candidate={candidate}
                        onClose={onClose}
                        onEdit={() => setIsEditModalOpen(true)}
                        isUpdating={isUpdating}
                        statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                    />
                    <ProfileModalContent
                        candidate={candidate}
                        age={age}
                        onStatusChange={onStatusChange}
                        onCancelInterview={onCancelInterview}
                        statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                        onScheduleInterview={onScheduleInterview}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
            <EditCandidateModal
                isOpen={isEditModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsEditModalOpen(false)}
                candidate={candidate}
                onSave={handleEditCandidate}
                isLoading={isUpdating}
            />
        </>
    );
};

export default CandidateProfileModal;

import React from 'react';

import CandidateTableRow from './candidate-table/CandidateTableRow';

const CandidateTable: React.FC<CandidateTableProps> = ({
  candidates,
  statusUpdateLoading,
  onViewCandidate,
  onScoreCandidate,
  onStatusChange,
  onEditCandidate,
  filters
}) => {
  const {
    sortField,
    sortDirection,
    handleSort,
    sortedCandidates,
    splitName,
    calculateAge,
  } = useCandidateTable(candidates, filters);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-200 dark:divide-gray-700">
        <CandidateTableHeader
          sortField={sortField}
          sortDirection={sortDirection}
          handleSort={handleSort}
        />
        <tbody className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 divide-y divide-gray-200 dark:divide-gray-700">
          {sortedCandidates.length > 0 ? (
            sortedCandidates.map((candidate) => (
              <CandidateTableRow
                key={candidate.id}
                candidate={candidate}
                splitName={splitName}
                calculateAge={calculateAge}
                onViewCandidate={onViewCandidate}

              <td colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12">

                    No candidates match your current filters. Try adjusting your search.

import React from 'react';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  PieChart,
  Pie,
  Cell,
  Tooltip as RechartsTooltip,
  Legend,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid
} from 'recharts';
import { DashboardChartsProps } from '@/types';

const STATUS_COLORS = ['#3B82F6', '#10B981', '#EF4444']; // Blue, Green, Red
const SCORE_COLORS = ['#EF4444', '#F59E0B', '#FCD34D', '#A7F3D0', '#10B981']; // Red to Green gradient

const DashboardCharts: React.FC<DashboardChartsProps> = ({
  statusChartData,
  scoreDistributionData
}) => {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const isDarkMode = theme === 'dark';

  const tickColor = isDarkMode ? '#9CA3AF' : '#64748b'; // gray-400 dark, gray-500 light
  const tooltipBackgroundColor = isDarkMode ? '#1F2937' : '#f8fafc'; // gray-800 dark, slate-50 light
  const tooltipBorderColor = isDarkMode ? '#374151' : '#e2e8f0'; // gray-700 dark, slate-200 light
  const legendColor = isDarkMode ? '#D1D5DB' : '#374151'; // gray-300 dark, gray-700 light
  return (
    <section className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
      {/* Status Distribution Pie Chart */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Candidate Status Distribution</h3>
          <div className="bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/50 p-2 rounded-lg">
            <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path fillRule="evenodd" d="M3 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1z" clipRule="evenodd" />
            </svg>
          </div>
        </div>
        {statusChartData.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="relative">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={350}>
              <PieChart>
                <Pie 
                  data={statusChartData} 
                  cx="50%" 
                  cy="50%" 
                  labelLine={false} 
                  outerRadius={120} 
                  innerRadius={60}
                  fill="#8884d8" 
                  dataKey="value" 
                  label={{ fill: legendColor }}
                >
                  {statusChartData.map((entry, index) => (
                    <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={STATUS_COLORS[index % STATUS_COLORS.length]} />
                  ))}
                </Pie>
                <RechartsTooltip 
                  contentStyle={{ 
                    backgroundColor: tooltipBackgroundColor, 
                    border: `1px solid ${tooltipBorderColor}`, 
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                  }} 
                />
                <Legend 
                  wrapperStyle={{ paddingTop: '20px', color: legendColor }}
                  iconType="circle"
                />
              </PieChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <svg className="w-16 h-16 mb-4 opacity-50" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path fillRule="evenodd" d="M3 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1z" clipRule="evenodd" />
            </svg>
            <p className="text-lg font-medium">No candidate data available</p>
            <p className="text-sm">Status distribution will appear here once candidates are added</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Score Distribution Bar Chart */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">AI Score Distribution</h3>
          <div className="bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/50 p-2 rounded-lg">
            <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path d="M9.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.07 3.292a1 1 0 00.95.69h3.462c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-2.8 2.034a1 1 0 00-.364 1.118l1.07 3.292c.3.921-.755 1.688-1.54 1.118l-2.8-2.034a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-2.8 2.034c-.784.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.07-3.292a1 1 0 00-.364-1.118L2.98 8.72c-.783-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h3.461a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.07-3.292z" />
            </svg>
          </div>
        </div>
        {scoreDistributionData.some(d => d.count > 0) ? (
          <div className="relative">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={350}>
              <BarChart data={scoreDistributionData} margin={{ top: 20, right: 30, left: 20, bottom: 5 }}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke={isDarkMode ? '#374151' : '#f1f5f9'} />
                <XAxis 
                  dataKey="name" 
                  stroke={tickColor}
                  fontSize={12}
                  tickLine={false}
                  axisLine={false}
                />
                <YAxis 
                  stroke={tickColor}
                  fontSize={12}
                  tickLine={false}
                  axisLine={false}
                  allowDecimals={false}
                />
                <RechartsTooltip 
                  contentStyle={{ 
                    backgroundColor: tooltipBackgroundColor, 
                    border: `1px solid ${tooltipBorderColor}`, 
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                  }} 
                  labelStyle={{ color: legendColor }}
                />
                <Bar 
                  dataKey="count" 
                  fill="#10B981"
                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                />
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <svg className="w-16 h-16 mb-4 opacity-50" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path d="M9.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.07 3.292a1 1 0 00.95.69h3.462c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-2.8 2.034a1 1 0 00-.364 1.118l1.07 3.292c.3.921-.755 1.688-1.54 1.118l-2.8-2.034a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-2.8 2.034c-.784.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.07-3.292a1 1 0 00-.364-1.118L2.98 8.72c-.783-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h3.461a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.07-3.292z" />
            </svg>
            <p className="text-lg font-medium">No scoring data available</p>
            <p className="text-sm">Score distribution will appear here once candidates are scored</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

export default DashboardCharts;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { useEditCandidateForm } from '@/hooks/useEditCandidateForm';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import EditModalHeader from './edit-candidate-modal/EditModalHeader';
import EditModalFooter from './edit-candidate-modal/EditModalFooter';
import EditCandidateForm from './edit-candidate-modal/EditCandidateForm';

interface EditCandidateModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  candidate: Candidate | null;
  onSave: (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => Promise<void>;
  isLoading: boolean;
}

const EditCandidateModal: React.FC<EditCandidateModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  candidate,
  onSave,
  isLoading
}) => {
  const {
    formData,
    errors,
    jobCategories,
    loadingJobs,
    availableSpecialties,
    filteredJobs,
    handleInputChange,
    handleCategoryChange,
    handleJobChange,
    handleSubmit,
  } = useEditCandidateForm(candidate, isOpen, onSave, onClose);

  if (!isOpen || !candidate) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-60 flex justify-center items-center z-50 p-4">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[95vh] flex flex-col">
        <EditModalHeader onClose={onClose} isLoading={isLoading} />
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-grow">
          <EditCandidateForm
            formData={formData}
            errors={errors}
            jobCategories={jobCategories}
            loadingJobs={loadingJobs}
            availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
            filteredJobs={filteredJobs}
            handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
            handleCategoryChange={handleCategoryChange}
            handleJobChange={handleJobChange}
            handleSubmit={handleSubmit}
            isLoading={isLoading}
          />
        </div>
        <EditModalFooter
          onClose={onClose}
          handleSubmit={handleSubmit}
          isLoading={isLoading}
          loadingJobs={loadingJobs}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default EditCandidateModal;

FILE: app/components/InterviewSchedulingModal.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { format, addDays } from 'date-fns';

import type { Candidate, InterviewSchedulingModalProps } from '@/types';

const InterviewSchedulingModal: React.FC<InterviewSchedulingModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  candidate,
  onSchedule,
  isLoading
}) => {
  const [interviewDate, setInterviewDate] = useState('');
  const [interviewTime, setInterviewTime] = useState('');
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(30); // Default 30 minutes
  const [adminEmail, setAdminEmail] = useState('wassim@internationalskills.fi');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');

  // Get tomorrow's date as minimum date
  const tomorrow = format(addDays(new Date(), 1), 'yyyy-MM-dd');

  // Generate time slots with better AM/PM display
  const generateTimeSlots = () => {
    const slots = [];
    for (let hour = 8; hour <= 17; hour++) {
      for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 30) {
        const time24 = `${hour.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${minute.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
        const hour12 = hour === 0 ? 12 : hour > 12 ? hour - 12 : hour;
        const ampm = hour >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
        const displayHour = hour12 === 0 ? 12 : hour12;
        const time12 = `${displayHour}:${minute.toString().padStart(2, '0')} ${ampm}`;
        slots.push({ 
          value: time24, 
          label: `${time12} (${time24})`,
          period: ampm,
          sortTime: hour * 60 + minute
        });
      }
    }
    return slots;
  };

  const timeSlots = generateTimeSlots();

  // Group time slots by AM/PM for better UX
  const morningSlots = timeSlots.filter(slot => slot.period === 'AM');
  const afternoonSlots = timeSlots.filter(slot => slot.period === 'PM');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (!candidate || !interviewDate || !interviewTime) {
      alert('Please fill in all required fields');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await onSchedule(
        candidate.id,
        interviewDate,
        interviewTime,
        duration,
        adminEmail || 'wassim@internationalskills.fi',
        notes
      );
      
      // Reset form on success
      setInterviewDate('');
      setInterviewTime('');
      setDuration(30);
      setNotes('');
      
    } catch (error) {
      // Error handling is done in the parent component
      console.error('Error in modal:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    setInterviewDate('');
    setInterviewTime('');
    setDuration(30);
    setNotes('');
    onClose();
  };

  if (!isOpen || !candidate) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center z-50">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-lg w-full mx-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">📅 Schedule Google Meet Interview</h3>
          <button
            onClick={handleClose}
            className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-300 focus:outline-none"
            disabled={isLoading}
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
        
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-blue-400 dark:border-blue-500">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">
            <strong>👤 Candidate:</strong> {candidate.fullName}
          </p>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">
            <strong>📧 Email:</strong> {candidate.email}
          </p>
          {candidate.jobTitleText && (
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">
              <strong>💼 Position:</strong> {candidate.jobTitleText}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="interviewDate" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
              📅 Interview Date *
            </label>
            <input
              type="date"
              id="interviewDate"
              value={interviewDate}
              onChange={(e) => setInterviewDate(e.target.value)}
              min={tomorrow}
              required
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              disabled={isLoading}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="interviewTime" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
              ⏰ Interview Time * (Finland Time)
            </label>
            <select
              id="interviewTime"
              value={interviewTime}
              onChange={(e) => setInterviewTime(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              disabled={isLoading}
            >
              <option value="">Select time</option>
              <optgroup label="🌅 Morning (AM)">
                {morningSlots.map(slot => (
                  <option key={slot.value} value={slot.value}>
                    {slot.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </optgroup>
              <optgroup label="🌆 Afternoon/Evening (PM)">
                {afternoonSlots.map(slot => (
                  <option key={slot.value} value={slot.value}>
                    {slot.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </optgroup>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="duration" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
              ⏱️ Duration *
            </label>
            <select
              id="duration"
              value={duration}
              onChange={(e) => setDuration(parseInt(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              disabled={isLoading}
            >
              <option value={15}>15 minutes</option>
              <option value={30}>30 minutes (recommended)</option>
              <option value={45}>45 minutes</option>
              <option value={60}>1 hour</option>
              <option value={90}>1.5 hours</option>
              <option value={120}>2 hours</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="adminEmail" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
              👨‍💼 Interviewer Email
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              id="adminEmail"
              value={adminEmail}
              onChange={(e) => setAdminEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              disabled={isLoading}
              placeholder="wassim@internationalskills.fi"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="notes" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
              📝 Additional Notes (optional)
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="notes"
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              rows={3}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              placeholder="Any additional information for the interview..."
              disabled={isLoading}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 p-4 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-green-400 dark:border-green-500">
            <div className="flex">
              <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                <span className="text-green-400 text-lg">✅</span>
              </div>
              <div className="ml-3">
                <p className="text-sm text-green-800 dark:text-green-300">
                  <strong>What happens next:</strong>
                </p>
                <ul className="text-sm text-green-700 dark:text-green-300/80 mt-2 space-y-1">
                  <li>• Google Calendar event will be created automatically</li>
                  <li>• Google Meet link will be generated</li>
                  <li>• Both candidate and interviewer receive email invitations</li>
                  <li>• Calendar reminders are set (24h and 1h before)</li>
                  <li>• Interview duration: {duration} minutes</li>
                </ul>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Show cancellation info if interview is already scheduled */}
          {candidate.status === 'interview_scheduled' && (
            <div className="bg-yellow-50 dark:bg-yellow-900/20 p-4 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-yellow-400 dark:border-yellow-500">
              <div className="flex">
                <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-yellow-400 text-lg">⚠️</span>
                </div>
                <div className="ml-3">
                  <p className="text-sm text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300">
                    <strong>Note:</strong> This candidate already has an interview scheduled. 
                    Scheduling a new interview will cancel the existing one and create a new Google Meet event.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex justify-end space-x-3 pt-4">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
              disabled={isLoading}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center"
              disabled={isLoading}
            >
              {isLoading ? (
                <>
                  <svg className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4 text-white" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4"></circle>
                    <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
                  </svg>
                  Scheduling...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  🎥 Schedule Google Meet Interview
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default InterviewSchedulingModal;

============================================================
FILE: app/components/JobManagement.tsx
============================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { JOB_CATEGORIES, JobCategory } from '@/lib/job-categories';
import { Job, JobManagementProps } from '@/types';

const inputFieldStyles = "block w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm placeholder-gray-400 dark:placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 sm:text-sm";

const JobManagement: React.FC<JobManagementProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<Job[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const [isAddingJob, setIsAddingJob] = useState(false);
  const [editingJobId, setEditingJobId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    description: '',
    category: '' as JobCategory | '',
    specialties: ['']
  });

  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('all');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<'title' | 'category' | 'createdAt' | 'isActive'>('createdAt');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState<'asc' | 'desc'>('desc');
  
  const [operationLoading, setOperationLoading] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const fetchJobs = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/jobs');
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to fetch jobs');
      setJobs(data.jobs || []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error fetching jobs:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to fetch jobs');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      fetchJobs();
      resetForm();
      setError(null);
      setSuccess(null);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (success || error) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        setSuccess(null);
        setError(null);
      }, 5000);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [success, error]);

  const addSpecialty = () => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, specialties: [...prev.specialties, ''] }));
  const removeSpecialty = (index: number) => {
    if (formData.specialties.length > 1) {
      setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, specialties: prev.specialties.filter((_, i) => i !== index) }));
    }
  };
  const updateSpecialty = (index: number, value: string) => {
    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, specialties: prev.specialties.map((spec, i) => i === index ? value : spec) }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const validSpecialties = formData.specialties.filter(spec => spec.trim() !== '');
    if (!formData.title.trim() || !formData.description.trim() || !formData.category || validSpecialties.length === 0) {
      setError('Please fill in all fields and add at least one specialty');
      return;
    }

    const operationKey = editingJobId || 'create';
    setOperationLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [operationKey]: true }));
    setError(null);
    setSuccess(null);

    try {
      const method = editingJobId ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
      const body = editingJobId 
        ? { jobId: editingJobId, ...formData, specialties: validSpecialties }
        : { ...formData, specialties: validSpecialties };

      const response = await fetch('/api/jobs', { method, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || `Failed to ${editingJobId ? 'update' : 'create'} job`);

      setSuccess(editingJobId ? 'Job updated successfully!' : 'Job created successfully!');
      resetForm();
      await fetchJobs();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error saving job:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : `Failed to ${editingJobId ? 'update' : 'create'} job`);
    } finally {
      setOperationLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [operationKey]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (jobId: string, jobTitle: string) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to permanently delete "${jobTitle}"? This action cannot be undone.`)) return;

    setOperationLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [`delete_${jobId}`]: true }));
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/jobs?jobId=${jobId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to delete job');
      setSuccess(`Job "${jobTitle}" deleted successfully!`);
      await fetchJobs();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete job');
    } finally {
      setOperationLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [`delete_${jobId}`]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleToggleStatus = async (jobId: string, currentStatus: boolean, jobTitle: string) => {
    const action = currentStatus ? 'deactivate' : 'activate';
    setOperationLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [`toggle_${jobId}`]: true }));
    try {
      const job = jobs.find(j => j.id === jobId);
      if (!job) throw new Error('Job not found');
      const response = await fetch('/api/jobs', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ jobId, isActive: !currentStatus, title: job.title, description: job.description, category: job.category, specialties: job.specialties || [] }),
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || `Failed to ${action} job`);
      setSuccess(`Job "${jobTitle}" ${action}d successfully!`);
      await fetchJobs();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : `Failed to ${action} job`);
    } finally {
      setOperationLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, [`toggle_${jobId}`]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleEdit = (job: Job) => {
    setFormData({ title: job.title, description: job.description, category: job.category, specialties: job.specialties && job.specialties.length > 0 ? job.specialties : [''] });
    setEditingJobId(job.id);
    setIsAddingJob(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccess(null);
  };

  const resetForm = () => {
    setFormData({ title: '', description: '', category: '', specialties: [''] });
    setEditingJobId(null);
    setIsAddingJob(false);
  };

  const filteredAndSortedJobs = React.useMemo(() => {
    let filtered = jobs;
    if (selectedCategory !== 'all') filtered = filtered.filter(job => job.category === selectedCategory);
    if (searchTerm.trim()) {
      const search = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(job => 
        job.title.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
        job.description.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
        job.category.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
        (job.specialties && job.specialties.some(spec => spec.toLowerCase().includes(search)))
      );
    }
    filtered.sort((a, b) => {
      let aValue, bValue;
      switch (sortBy) {
        case 'title': aValue = a.title.toLowerCase(); bValue = b.title.toLowerCase(); break;
        case 'category': aValue = a.category.toLowerCase(); bValue = b.category.toLowerCase(); break;
        case 'isActive': aValue = a.isActive ? 1 : 0; bValue = b.isActive ? 1 : 0; break;
        default: aValue = a.createdAt?.toMillis ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0; bValue = b.createdAt?.toMillis ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0; break;
      }
      if (aValue < bValue) return sortOrder === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
      if (aValue > bValue) return sortOrder === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
      return 0;
    });
    return filtered;
  }, [jobs, selectedCategory, searchTerm, sortBy, sortOrder]);

  const jobsByCategory = React.useMemo(() => {
    return JOB_CATEGORIES.reduce((acc, category) => {
      acc[category] = jobs.filter(job => job.category === category);
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<JobCategory, Job[]>);
  }, [jobs]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-60 flex justify-center items-center z-50 p-4">
      <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-5xl w-full max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Job Management</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors p-2 rounded-full -mr-2 -mt-2">
            <svg className="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-y-auto pr-2 -mr-2 flex-grow">
          {error && <Alert type="error" message={error} />}
          {success && <Alert type="success" message={success} />}

          {!isAddingJob && (
            <div className="mb-6">
              <button onClick={() => setIsAddingJob(true)} disabled={loading} className="btn btn-primary" data-testid="add-job-button">
                <svg className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 6v6m0 0v6m0-6h6m-6 0H6" /></svg>
                <span>Add New Job</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {isAddingJob && (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg mb-6 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-md" data-testid="job-form">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">{editingJobId ? 'Edit Job' : 'Add New Job'}</h3>
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <InputField label="Category" required>
                    <select value={formData.category} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, category: e.target.value as JobCategory })} className={inputFieldStyles} required>
                      <option value="">Select a category</option>
                      {JOB_CATEGORIES.map(category => <option key={category} value={category}>{category}</option>)}
                    </select>
                  </InputField>
                  <InputField label="Job Title" required>
                    <input type="text" value={formData.title} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })} className={inputFieldStyles} placeholder="e.g., Senior Developer" required />
                  </InputField>
                </div>
                <InputField label="Job Description" required>
                  <textarea value={formData.description} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, description: e.target.value })} className={inputFieldStyles} placeholder="Describe the role..." rows={4} required />
                </InputField>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Specialties <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {formData.specialties.map((specialty, index) => (
                      <div key={index} className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <input type="text" value={specialty} onChange={(e) => updateSpecialty(index, e.target.value)} className={`${inputFieldStyles} flex-1`} placeholder={`Specialty ${index + 1}`} required />
                        {formData.specialties.length > 1 && (
                          <button type="button" onClick={() => removeSpecialty(index)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-100 dark:hover:bg-red-900/50 rounded-full transition-colors">
                            <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" /></svg>
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    <button type="button" onClick={addSpecialty} className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-800 dark:hover:text-blue-300 font-medium">
                      <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 6v6m0 0v6m0-6h6m-6 0H6" /></svg>
                      <span>Add Specialty</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-4 pt-2">
                  <button type="submit" disabled={operationLoading[editingJobId || 'create']} className="btn btn-primary" data-testid="create-job-button">
                    {operationLoading[editingJobId || 'create'] ? 'Saving...' : (editingJobId ? 'Update Job' : 'Create Job')}
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={resetForm} className="btn btn-secondary">Cancel</button>
                </div>
              </form>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-md">
            <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              <InputField label="Search Jobs">
                <input type="text" value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} className={inputFieldStyles} placeholder="Search..." />
              </InputField>
              <InputField label="Filter by Category">
                <select value={selectedCategory} onChange={(e) => setSelectedCategory(e.target.value)} className={inputFieldStyles}>
                  <option value="all">All Categories ({jobs.length})</option>
                  {JOB_CATEGORIES.map(category => <option key={category} value={category}>{category} ({jobsByCategory[category]?.length || 0})</option>)}
                </select>
              </InputField>
              <InputField label="Sort by">
                <div className="flex space-x-2">
                  <select value={sortBy} onChange={(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value as any)} className={`${inputFieldStyles} flex-1`}>
                    <option value="createdAt">Date Created</option>
                    <option value="title">Title</option>
                    <option value="category">Category</option>
                    <option value="isActive">Status</option>
                  </select>
                  <button onClick={() => setSortOrder(sortOrder === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc')} className="p-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700">
                    {sortOrder === 'asc' ? '↑' : '↓'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </InputField>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3">
              {loading && !isAddingJob && <p className="dark:text-gray-300">Loading jobs...</p>}
              {!loading && filteredAndSortedJobs.length === 0 && (
                <div className="text-center py-10">
                  <p className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">No jobs match your filters.</p>
                  <button onClick={() => { setSearchTerm(''); setSelectedCategory('all'); }} className="mt-2 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline">Clear filters</button>
                </div>
              )}
              {filteredAndSortedJobs.map((job) => <JobListItem key={job.id} job={job} onEdit={handleEdit} onToggleStatus={handleToggleStatus} onDelete={handleDelete} loading={operationLoading} />)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const InputField: React.FC<{ label: string; children: React.ReactNode; required?: boolean }> = ({ label, children, required = false }) => (
  <div>
    <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">{label} {required && <span className="text-red-500">*</span>}</label>
    {children}
  </div>
);

const Alert: React.FC<{ type: 'error' | 'success'; message: string }> = ({ type, message }) => {
  const colors = {
    error: { bg: 'bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20', border: 'border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30', text: 'text-red-800 dark:text-red-300', icon: 'text-red-400 dark:text-red-400' },
    success: { bg: 'bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20', border: 'border-green-200 dark:border-green-500/30', text: 'text-green-800 dark:text-green-300', icon: 'text-green-400 dark:text-green-400' },
  };
  const style = colors[type];
  return (
    <div className={`${style.bg} border ${style.border} rounded-lg p-4 mb-4`}>
      <div className="flex">
        <div className="flex-shrink-0">
          <svg className={`h-5 w-5 ${style.icon}`} viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor">
            {type === 'error' ? <path fillRule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zM8.707 7.293a1 1 0 00-1.414 1.414L8.586 10l-1.293 1.293a1 1 0 101.414 1.414L10 11.414l1.293 1.293a1 1 0 001.414-1.414L11.414 10l1.293-1.293a1 1 0 00-1.414-1.414L10 8.586 8.707 7.293z" clipRule="evenodd" /> : <path fillRule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zm3.707-9.293a1 1 0 00-1.414-1.414L9 10.586 7.707 9.293a1 1 0 00-1.414 1.414l2 2a1 1 0 001.414 0l4-4z" clipRule="evenodd" />}
          </svg>
        </div>
        <div className="ml-3"><p className={style.text}>{message}</p></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface JobListItemProps {
    job: Job;
    onEdit: (job: Job) => void;
    onToggleStatus: (jobId: string, currentStatus: boolean, jobTitle: string) => void;
    onDelete: (jobId: string, jobTitle: string) => void;
    loading: Record<string, boolean>;
}

const JobListItem: React.FC<JobListItemProps> = ({ job, onEdit, onToggleStatus, onDelete, loading }) => (
  <div className="border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-4 hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow bg-white dark:bg-gray-800">
    <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
      <div className="flex-1">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
          <h4 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{job.title}</h4>
          <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs rounded-full font-semibold ${job.isActive ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 dark:bg-green-900/50 dark:text-green-300' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300'}`}>{job.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-semibold mb-2">{job.category}</p>
        <p className="text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed mb-3">{job.description}</p>
        {job.specialties && job.specialties.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {job.specialties.map((specialty, index) => <span key={index} className="px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-md">{specialty}</span>)}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {job.createdAt && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500">Created: {job.createdAt.toDate?.().toLocaleDateString() || 'Unknown'}</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 ml-4">
        <ActionButton onClick={() => onEdit(job)} disabled={loading[`edit_${job.id}`]} icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15.232 5.232l3.536 3.536m-2.036-5.036a2.5 2.5 0 113.536 3.536L6.5 21.036H3v-3.5L16.732 3.732z" /></svg>} srText="Edit" color="blue" />
        <ActionButton onClick={() => onToggleStatus(job.id, job.isActive, job.title)} disabled={loading[`toggle_${job.id}`]} icon={job.isActive ? <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M18.364 18.364A9 9 0 005.636 5.636m12.728 12.728A9 9 0 015.636 5.636m12.728 12.728L5.636 5.636" /></svg> : <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" /></svg>} srText={job.isActive ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'} color={job.isActive ? 'yellow' : 'green'} />
        <ActionButton onClick={() => onDelete(job.id, job.title)} disabled={loading[`delete_${job.id}`]} icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" /></svg>} srText="Delete" color="red" />
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
);

interface ActionButtonProps {
    onClick: () => void;
    icon: React.ReactElement;
    srText: string;
    color: string;
    disabled?: boolean;
}

const ActionButton: React.FC<ActionButtonProps> = ({ onClick, icon, srText, color, disabled }) => (
    <button onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled} className={`p-2 rounded-full text-${color}-600 dark:text-${color}-400 hover:bg-${color}-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-${color}-500 transition-colors`}>
        <span className="sr-only">{srText}</span>
        {icon}
    </button>
);

export default JobManagement;
