// src/components/ScoringModal.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Timestamp } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { Candidate, Job, ScoringModalProps } from '@/types';

const ScoringModal: React.FC<ScoringModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, candidate, onSubmit, isLoading, error }) => {
    const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<Job[]>([]);
    const [selectedJobId, setSelectedJobId] = useState('');
    const [selectedJob, setSelectedJob] = useState<Job | null>(null);
    const [loadingJobs, setLoadingJobs] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            loadJobs();
        }
    }, [isOpen]);

    const loadJobs = async () => {
        setLoadingJobs(true);
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/jobs');
            const data = await response.json();
            if (response.ok) {
                const activeJobs = data.jobs.filter((job: Job) => job.isActive);
                setJobs(activeJobs);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error loading jobs:', error);
        } finally {
            setLoadingJobs(false);
        }
    };

    const handleJobSelection = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const jobId = e.target.value;
        setSelectedJobId(jobId);
        const job = jobs.find(j => j.id === jobId);
        setSelectedJob(job || null);
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (selectedJob) {
            onSubmit(selectedJob.id, selectedJob.title, selectedJob.description);
        }
    };

    if (!isOpen || !candidate) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center z-50">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">Score Candidate: {candidate.fullName}</h2>
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
                    <div className="mb-4">
                        <label htmlFor="jobSelection" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">
                            Select Job Position <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                        </label>
                        {loadingJobs ? (
                            <div className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Loading jobs...</div>
                        ) : (
                            <select
                                id="jobSelection"
                                value={selectedJobId}
                                onChange={handleJobSelection}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 sm:text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                                required
                            >
                                <option value="">Select a job position</option>
                                {jobs.map((job) => (
                                    <option key={job.id} value={job.id}>
                                        {job.title}
                                    </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {selectedJob && (
                        <div className="mb-4 p-4 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30 rounded-lg">
                            <h4 className="font-medium text-blue-900 dark:text-blue-300 mb-2">Job Description:</h4>
                            <p className="text-sm text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300/80 whitespace-pre-wrap">{selectedJob.description}</p>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {error && (
                        <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-500/30 rounded-lg">
                            <p className="text-red-600 dark:text-red-300 text-sm">Error: {error}</p>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3">
                        <button 
                            type="button" 
                            onClick={onClose} 
                            disabled={isLoading} 
                            className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-300 dark:bg-gray-600 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 rounded hover:bg-gray-400 dark:hover:bg-gray-500 disabled:opacity-50"
                        >
                            Cancel
                        </button>
                        <button 
                            type="submit" 
                            disabled={isLoading || !selectedJob} 
                            className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                        >
                            {isLoading ? 'Scoring...' : 'Score Candidate'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ScoringModal;

FILE: app/components/StatsCards.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Candidate, Stats, StatsCardsProps } from '@/types';

interface StatCardProps {
  title: string;
  value: string | number;
  icon: React.ReactElement<{ className?: string }>;
  color: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  cardType?: 'primary' | 'secondary';
}

// A new, reusable StatCard component for a consistent look.
const StatCard: React.FC<StatCardProps> = ({ title, value, icon, color, children, cardType = 'primary' }) => {
  const colorClasses = {
    primary: {
      iconBg: `bg-${color}-100 dark:bg-${color}-900/50`,
      iconText: `text-${color}-600 dark:text-${color}-400`,
      valueText: `text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100`,
    },
    secondary: {
      iconBg: `bg-transparent`,
      iconText: `text-${color}-500 dark:text-${color}-400`,
      valueText: `text-${color}-600 dark:text-${color}-400`,
    }
  };

  const styles = colorClasses[cardType];

  return (
    <div className={`bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-xl shadow-md transition-shadow hover:shadow-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{title}</h3>
          <p className={`text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold mt-1 ${styles.valueText}`}>{value}</p>
          {children && <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-1">{children}</div>}
        </div>
        <div className={`p-3 rounded-full ${styles.iconBg}`}>
          {React.cloneElement(icon, { className: `w-6 h-6 ${styles.iconText}` })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


const StatsCards: React.FC<StatsCardsProps> = ({ stats, candidates }) => {
  const averageScore = candidates.length > 0 
    ? candidates.filter(c => c.score !== null).reduce((sum, c) => sum + (c.score || 0), 0) / candidates.filter(c => c.score !== null).length
    : 0;

  const highScorers = candidates.filter(c => (c.score || 0) >= 80).length;

  const calculateConversionRate = () => {
    if (stats.submitted === 0) return '0.0';
    const conversionRate = (stats.scheduled / stats.submitted) * 100;
    if (conversionRate > 100) {
      console.warn('Warning: Scheduled count is higher than submitted count. This may indicate data inconsistency.');
      return '100.0';
    }
    return conversionRate.toFixed(1);
  };

  const interviewCompletionRate = stats.scheduled > 0 ? ((stats.approved / stats.scheduled) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';

  const mainStats = [
    { title: 'Total Candidates', value: stats.total, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M17 20h5v-2a3 3 0 00-5.356-1.857M17 20H7m10 0v-2c0-.653-.124-1.283-.356-1.857M7 20H2v-2a3 3 0 015.356-1.857M7 20v-2c0-.653.124-1.283.356-1.857m0 0a5.002 5.002 0 019.288 0M15 7a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0zm6 3a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0zM7 10a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0z" /></svg>, color: 'indigo' },
    { title: 'Submitted', value: stats.submitted, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" /></svg>, color: 'blue', children: <>{stats.total > 0 ? `${((stats.submitted / stats.total) * 100).toFixed(1)}% of total` : '0.0% of total'}</> },
    { title: 'Interview Scheduled', value: stats.scheduled, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z" /></svg>, color: 'green', children: <>{stats.total > 0 ? `${((stats.scheduled / stats.total) * 100).toFixed(1)}% of total` : '0.0% of total'}</> },
    { title: 'Rejected', value: stats.rejected, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M18.364 18.364A9 9 0 005.636 5.636m12.728 12.728A9 9 0 015.636 5.636m12.728 12.728L5.636 5.636" /></svg>, color: 'red', children: <>{stats.total > 0 ? `${((stats.rejected / stats.total) * 100).toFixed(1)}% of total` : '0.0% of total'}</> },
  ];

  const secondaryStats = [
    { title: 'Average AI Score', value: averageScore > 0 ? averageScore.toFixed(1) : '0.0', icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M11.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.519 4.674a1 1 0 00.95.69h4.915c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-3.976 2.888a1 1 0 00-.363 1.118l1.518 4.674c.3.922-.755 1.688-1.539 1.118l-3.975-2.888a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-3.976 2.888c-.783.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.518-4.674a1 1 0 00-.363-1.118l-3.976-2.888c-.784-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h4.914a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.519-4.674z" /></svg>, color: 'purple', children: <>{candidates.filter(c => c.score !== null).length} candidates scored</> },
    { title: 'High Scorers (80+)', value: highScorers, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 7h8m0 0v8m0-8l-8 8-4-4-6 6" /></svg>, color: 'yellow', children: <>Excellent candidates</> },
    { title: 'Conversion Rate', value: `${calculateConversionRate()}%`, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 17h8m0 0V9m0 8l-8-8-4 4-6-6" /></svg>, color: 'teal', children: <>Submitted → Scheduled</> },
    { title: 'Interview Success', value: `${interviewCompletionRate}%`, icon: <svg fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" /></svg>, color: 'emerald', children: <>Scheduled → Approved</> },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="mb-8">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-6">
        {mainStats.map(stat => <StatCard key={stat.title} {...stat} />)}
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
        {secondaryStats.map(stat => <StatCard key={stat.title} {...stat} cardType="secondary" />)}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

export default StatsCards;

"use client";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Inter } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";
import { AuthProvider } from "@/hooks/useAuth"; // Import AuthProvider
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import Loader from "./components/ui/Loader";
import { ThemeProvider } from "./providers";

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ["latin"] });

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setLoading(false), 2000); // Simulate a loading time
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);

  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <body className={inter.className}>
        <ThemeProvider
          attribute="class"
          defaultTheme="system"
          enableSystem
          disableTransitionOnChange
        >
          {loading && <Loader />}
          <AuthProvider> {/* Wrap children with AuthProvider */}
            {children}
          </AuthProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

FILE: app/page.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ThemeSwitcher } from './components/ui/ThemeSwitcher';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';

// Helper component for social media icons in the footer
const SocialIcon = ({ href, children }: { href: string, children: React.ReactNode }) => (
  <a href={href} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors">
    {children}
  </a>
);

export default function LandingPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const { theme } = useTheme();

  const handleEmailSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!email.trim()) {
      setError('Please enter your email address');
      return;
    }

    // Basic email validation first
    const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
    if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
      setError('Please enter a valid email address');
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();

      // 🔥 STEP 1: Check database first (FREE - no API cost)
      console.log('Step 1: Checking database for existing candidate...');
      const checkResponse = await fetch('/api/check-candidate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: normalizedEmail }),
      });

      const checkResult = await checkResponse.json();
      
      if (!checkResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error(checkResult.error || 'Failed to check candidate status');
      }

      // If candidate exists in database, skip email verification
      if (checkResult.exists && checkResult.count > 0) {
        console.log('✅ Existing candidate found in database - redirecting to choices');
        router.push(`/candidate-choices?email=${encodeURIComponent(normalizedEmail)}`);
        return;
      }

      // 🔥 STEP 2: Only verify email if candidate doesn't exist (COSTS MONEY)
      console.log('Step 2: New email - verifying with external API...');
      const verifyResponse = await fetch('/api/verify-email', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: normalizedEmail }),
      });

      const verifyResult = await verifyResponse.json();
      
      if (!verifyResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error(verifyResult.error || 'Email verification failed');
      }

      if (!verifyResult.isValid) {
        setError('Please enter a valid email address that exists');
        return;
      }

      // 🔥 STEP 3: Email is valid and new - redirect to form
      console.log('✅ Email verified - redirecting to application form');
      router.push(`/candidate-form?email=${encodeURIComponent(normalizedEmail)}`);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Email submission error:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'An unexpected error occurred');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-900 font-sans">
      {/* Header */}
      <header className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 shadow-sm sticky top-0 z-20">
          <div className="bg-[#003A70] text-white text-xs py-1.5">
              <div className="max-w-screen-xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 flex justify-center md:justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="flex items-center">
                      <span className="text-yellow-400 mr-2 text-base">⚠️</span>
                      <span>Découvrez les dernières opportunités!</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="hidden md:flex items-center space-x-6">
                      <a href="tel:+358452644966" className="flex items-center space-x-2 hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors">
                          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" className="h-4 w-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M3 5a2 2 0 012-2h3.28a1 1 0 01.948.684l1.498 4.493a1 1 0 01-.502 1.21l-2.257 1.13a11.042 11.042 0 005.516 5.516l1.13-2.257a1 1 0 011.21-.502l4.493 1.498a1 1 0 01.684.949V19a2 2 0 01-2 2h-1C9.716 21 3 14.284 3 6V5z" /></svg>
                          <span>+358 452644966</span>
                      </a>

                    Email Address <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="email"

                    id="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-6 py-4 text-lg border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-xl shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-colors"
                    placeholder="your.email@example.com"

                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="w-full px-8 py-4 bg-[#003A70] text-white font-bold text-lg tracking-wide rounded-xl shadow-lg hover:bg-[#002B54] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-[#003A70] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all transform hover:scale-[1.02]"
                  disabled={isLoading}
                >
                  {isLoading ? (

                </p>

              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-[#003A70]/10 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">

                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M21 13.255A23.931 23.931 0 0112 15c-3.183 0-6.22-.62-9-1.745M16 6V4a2 2 0 00-2-2h-4a2 2 0 00-2-2v2m8 0V6a2 2 0 012 2v6a2 2 0 01-2 2H8a2 2 0 01-2-2V8a2 2 0 012-2V6" />
                </svg>
              </div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Global Opportunities</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">Access international job opportunities across various industries</p>
            </div>

            <div className="text-center space-y-3">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-[#003A70]/10 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                <svg className="w-8 h-8 text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                </svg>
              </div>

"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { ThemeProvider as NextThemesProvider } from "next-themes";

type ThemeProviderProps = React.ComponentProps<typeof NextThemesProvider>;

export function ThemeProvider({ children, ...props }: ThemeProviderProps) {
  return <NextThemesProvider {...props}>{children}</NextThemesProvider>;
}


============================================================
FILE: hooks/__tests__/useAuth.test.tsx
============================================================
import { renderHook, act } from '@testing-library/react';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from '../useAuth';
import { auth } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut, onAuthStateChanged } from 'firebase/auth';
import { User } from 'firebase/auth';

jest.mock('@/lib/firebase', () => ({
  auth: {},
}));

jest.mock('firebase/auth', () => ({
  signInWithEmailAndPassword: jest.fn(),
  signOut: jest.fn(),
  onAuthStateChanged: jest.fn(),
}));

const mockOnAuthStateChanged = onAuthStateChanged as jest.Mock;

describe('useAuth', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('should return the initial state', () => {
    mockOnAuthStateChanged.mockImplementation((auth, callback) => {
      callback(null);
      return jest.fn();
    });

    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), {
      wrapper: AuthProvider,
    });

    expect(result.current.user).toBeNull();
    expect(result.current.loading).toBe(false);
    expect(result.current.error).toBeNull();
  });

  it('should update user when auth state changes', () => {
    const mockUser = { uid: '123', email: 'test@example.com' } as User;
    
    act(() => {
      mockOnAuthStateChanged.mockImplementation((auth, callback) => {
        callback(mockUser);
        return jest.fn();
      });
    });

    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), {
      wrapper: AuthProvider,
    });

    expect(result.current.user).toEqual(mockUser);
    expect(result.current.loading).toBe(false);
  });

  it('should sign in a user successfully', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), {
      wrapper: AuthProvider,
    });

    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.signIn('test@example.com', 'password');
    });

    expect(signInWithEmailAndPassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      auth,
      'test@example.com',
      'password'
    );
    expect(result.current.error).toBeNull();
  });

  it('should handle sign in failure', async () => {
    const error = new Error('Invalid credentials');
    (signInWithEmailAndPassword as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValue(error);
    const consoleErrorSpy = jest.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), {
      wrapper: AuthProvider,
    });

    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.signIn('test@example.com', 'wrong-password');
    });

    expect(result.current.error).toBe(error.message);
    consoleErrorSpy.mockRestore();
  });

  it('should sign out a user successfully', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), {
      wrapper: AuthProvider,
    });

    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.signOut();
    });

    expect(signOut).toHaveBeenCalledWith(auth);
    expect(result.current.user).toBeNull();
    expect(result.current.error).toBeNull();
  });

  it('should handle sign out failure', async () => {
    const error = new Error('Sign out failed');
    (signOut as jest.Mock).mockRejectedValue(error);
    const consoleErrorSpy = jest.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAuth(), {
      wrapper: AuthProvider,
    });

    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.signOut();
    });

    expect(result.current.error).toBe(error.message);
    consoleErrorSpy.mockRestore();
  });
});


============================================================
FILE: hooks/useAuth.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useContext, createContext, ReactNode } from 'react';
// import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // Removed as it's unused here
import { auth } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { 
  onAuthStateChanged, 
  signOut as firebaseSignOut, 
  signInWithEmailAndPassword, 
  User 
} from "firebase/auth";
import { AuthContextType } from '@/types';

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // const router = useRouter(); // Removed as it's unused here

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
      setUser(user);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const signIn = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      // Redirect is handled by the component using the hook based on user state
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Firebase Sign In Error:", err);
      // Type assertion for error object
      if (err instanceof Error) {
          setError(err.message || "Failed to sign in");
      } else {
          setError("Failed to sign in: An unknown error occurred");
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const signOut = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await firebaseSignOut(auth);
      setUser(null);
      // Redirect is handled where signOut is called, e.g., in the dashboard component
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Firebase Sign Out Error:", err);
      // Type assertion for error object
      if (err instanceof Error) {
          setError(err.message || "Failed to sign out");
      } else {
          setError("Failed to sign out: An unknown error occurred");
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const value = {
    user,
    loading,
    error,
    signIn,
    signOut,
  };

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextType => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  }
  return context;
};
